Trousers (TR-1): cotton-stretch twill 280 g/m², matte, blue-black #25272C (approx L*16), in the shirt's cast so the two read as one mass (verified: 'The man is dressed in all black, as her black hole'). Boxer briefs: cotton-modal jersey, black #232428, plain waistband, no lettering. Socks: cotton-nylon, black #232427. Belt: 3 cm cowhide, brown-black #2A2624 (approx L*16), 5 holes 2.5 cm apart, buckle in matte black oxide #2B2C2E approx. Older than the story: a deep crease with cracked grain at the middle (third) hole, where it fastened before he got heavier, and the tip worn pale #5A4A40. To rebuild it, bend and sand the leather. Derbies (PR-41): calf leather, brown-black #2A2624, stitched welt, black sole edge, no coloured sole, no logo. Third-night state, SC04-SC15: worn since the morning and sat in at the bar since early evening. The collar points have gone soft, tips lifted 6-8 mm off the shirt front; horizontal crumple creases at both inner elbows; a fan of sit creases across the trouser lap. There is no sharp fold line anywhere: the fold creases are JEAN_L02's, made by her. To rebuild: stays out, the actor sits in SH-1 and TR-1 for 40 minutes with his elbows bent before the first take, and the steamer never touches the elbows or the lap. K08-B tee: cotton single jersey 240 g/m², solid, matte, blue-black #26282D (approx L*16). K08-C overshirt: brushed cotton drill 300 g/m², solid, blue-black #292B30 (approx L*17), point collar, 5 matte black corozo buttons #26272B, two patch hip pockets, no chest pocket. Bar-set risk: #24262B (approx L*15) on the counter #3A2418 (approx L*17, C*15, h 54°) inside the #1A120D room (approx L*6). Value alone won't separate him from the counter. The costume gives his blue cast about 140° from the room's brown-black (weak at C*4), matte brushed twill against the counter's satin varnish, the collar edge with its lifted points and the wedge of deep skin at the open neck, and from SC05 Venus's bone jacket #D6CCBA (approx L*82) beside him. The rest is the DP's. The route's #111111 is the graded read; in a neutral grade the fabric is #24262B. Nothing branded.
- **fit notes**: 184 cm, US men's L, 36 waist. Shirt: shoulder seam at the shoulder point. Top button open, so the collar stand sits 1-2 cm clear of his neck and the soft points spread and lift. Cuffs at the wrist bone. Tucked 10 cm deep; the front blouses 2 cm over the belt where his belly sits. The button line pulls across the belly but never gapes: a hidden snap sits between the 4th and 5th buttons. Third-night creases land in a 10 cm band at each inner elbow and across the trouser lap from the hip crease to mid-thigh, and they still show standing (SC08 exit, SC10). Trousers: waist 3 cm below the navel and snug (he has outgrown them), 20 cm leg opening, half break on the Derbies, room in the seat to lift and carry VENUS (SC14). Belt on hole 1, 5 cm out from the old crease. SC10: shoes off on the mat (PR-41 S2), socks on. SC15: under the sheet, a skin-matched modesty garment (#6E4631 approx), never seen; the sheet sits at the waist. K08-B tee: shoulder seam at the point, 8 cm ease, hem at the hip, covering the belt. K08-C overshirt: shoulder seam 1 cm past the point, sleeves to the wrist bone, hem at the hip bone, fronts hanging open. Builds SH-1 and TR-1 are listed under JEAN_L02.
- **accessories**: Placket bare SC04-SC15: nothing hooked, clipped or tacked to it. PR-04 is not on him; it rides in PR-05's outside pocket until SC17 (film director's ruling; set-owned; blocker 1).; Nothing else worn: no watch, ring, chain or bracelet. Neck and hands bare. His right hand holds his phone and stands the straw.; Set-owned: PR-35 in his right front trouser pocket, no bulge above the pocket line.; K08 prints: no sunglasses, jewellery, bag strap or anything hanging at the neckline.
- **hair makeup**: Skin: deep, warm undertone, #6E4631 approx at the cheekbone (casting hex). No base. Left uncovered: the pores on the nose and cheeks, the two forehead lines, the crow's feet, the under-eye puffiness from three nights of drinking, and the forehead shine. Hair: black #17110E approx, dense tight 4A curls. The top is 12-15 cm stretched, about 5 cm as it sits, picked out at the crown with a wide-tooth pick and set with a light leave-in, no gel, so the top shifts as one mass and the crown ends flutter in wind (A16 test). Sides and back tapered to 1 cm, no part, no razor line. Squared hairline, the left temple receding further than the right. Beard: close at 3 mm, hard razored cheek line, neckline 2 cm above the Adam's apple; the 3 mm moustache joins the beard. The lentil-sized mole on the left side of the neck stays uncovered. Hair, beard and mole are the same in every K08 print. Never lightened, de-aged or loosened in curl by makeup, the grade or the 35mm look.
- **continuity locks**: [SC04 and SC08 window wides] One unbroken black mass from collar to shoes, no jacket; the only breaks are the wedge of deep skin at the open collar and the rounded picked-out top. In SC08 the bone jacket sits beside him: black and bone.; [wide] All black head to toe, shirt tucked with a 2 cm blouse over the belt, belt showing. No jewellery, no watch.; [close-up, SC04-SC15] Top button open, second fastened (the reverse of JEAN_L02). Collar tips lifted 6-8 mm off the shirt. Mole on the LEFT side of the neck above the collar.; [medium, SC04-SC15] Crumple creases at both inner elbows; no fold crease on the chest.; [medium, SC04-SC15, and every K08 print] Placket or neckline bare: no sunglasses, nothing hooked or tacked. No sunglasses on his face in any print.; [every K08 print] Black head to toe, solid and unmarked, and only one of K08-A, K08-B or K08-C.; [insert, belt] Fastened on hole 1; the cracked crease at the middle hole sits 5 cm past the buckle.; [close-up] Beard 3 mm with a hard cheek line. Top picked out to about 5 cm as it sits; sides 1 cm.; [SC12-SC13 inserts] No white fibre on the black shirt or trousers; lint-rolled between takes.; [SC15 wide] Clothes off, on the floor, then folded square on the white chair with nothing on top. Bare back and arms, sheet at the waist.

### JEAN_L02 (JEAN)

- **garment silhouette**: Story: the same shirt, trousers, belt, socks, briefs and Derbies as JEAN_L01; only the production builds differ (see fit_notes). SC16, morning: undressed under the sheet, face-down, right arm off the bed, until she rolls him onto his back and sets his face toward her on the pillow. SC17, the apartment at 21 °C: VENUS dresses him, seats him in the armchair and puts on PR-04, taken out of PR-05's outside pocket. V01, the open car in the desert, 27 °C falling to 17 °C: long sleeves, because she dressed him. SC18: the motel at night. He is dressed her way, not his: every button fastened, including the top one, no tie. Cuffs buttoned. Tucked flat, no blouse over the belt. Belt on hole 1, where he wore it. The fastened top button is the change the camera sees: it sits at the collar her hand straightens in V01-B02, and he would never have done it himself. A body double wears this exact look for the SH14 and SH19 practical plates (film director's shot IDs; see fit_notes and hair_makeup).
- **materials colors**: As JEAN_L01: shirt in unmercerised, lightly brushed cotton twill 150 g/m², true matte, blue-black #24262B; trousers in stretch twill 280 g/m², blue-black #25272C; belt and Derbies brown-black #2A2624; buckle black oxide #2B2C2E approx; socks #232427; briefs #232428. The L01 sit creases at the inner elbows and across the lap stay, because the clothes are never pressed. Every build gets them from the same 40-minute seated wear. New in L02: the fold state from PR-55, pressed into SH-2, SH-3 and SH-4. It is one sharp horizontal crease across the chest 12 cm below the collar and two vertical creases 10 cm in from the side seams. The collar points now lie flat against the shirt, because the fastened top button closes the collar; SH-2, SH-3 and SH-4 points carry a light fusible so they stay down in the wind. The brushed nap grabs white bouclé fibre from PR-44 in the SC17 held frame, so it is lint-rolled between takes. Build hardware: SH-4 snaps in flat black oxide #26272B approx, matte; TR-2 invisible zip, black nylon coil #232428. Desert dust is set-owned PR-57, #C9A45C approx, matte. C1: a film on the shoulders and collar points at V01-B01. C2: her hand brushes the points back to blue-black at B02, shoulders still filmed. C3: a fresh film on the points from B05, glinting under the video light, through B08, B11 and SC18.
- **fit notes**: Builds: all in the L01 fabrics and buttons. He is seated or slumped in every L02 frame. SH-1, the live hero: JEAN_L01 in SC04-SC15, then PR-56 S1-S2 (on the floor, then folded). Collar band 43 cm; its top button is never fastened on screen. SH-2, the dead hero: collar band cut 1 cm larger (44 cm), so the fastened top button closes flat on his thick neck with the mole still clear below the ear. It serves the SC17 held frame in the armchair, V01-B01 and B03-B14, V02, S01 and SC18. SH-3, the dust repeat: identical to SH-2, pre-dusted to C1 by production design (PR-57), swapped in for each retake of V01-B02 and V02-B01, so every brush take starts from C1 while SH-2 holds its later state. SH-4, the dressing double: as SH-2, with a centre-back split from the collar band to the hem, closed by 7 flat snaps under a 3 cm underlap, unseen from front and sides. Used in SC17 while she dresses him on screen, and for loading him into the car seat and the motel chair; never in V01 0-56 s, where the phone sees the back of his collar. TR-1, the hero trousers: JEAN_L01, SC04-SC15, including the SC14 carry. TR-2, the dressing double: an invisible zip in the right side seam from waistband to hem, same waist as TR-1, 2 cm more seat ease so the seat seam doesn't strain as he slumps; used in every L02 frame, its right seam against the passenger door in V01. Belts x2: the hero and a matched copy, crease and pale tip rebuilt the same way. Derbies: the hero pair, plus a pair half a size up with laces pre-set loose, for SC17 on. Fit: slumped against the door, cheek on the headrest, the collar stands clear of the jaw, so collar and jaw read as two shapes (V01 0-42 s). Tucked 10 cm deep and flat all round; nothing rides up or gapes. Cuffs buttoned at the wrist bone. Seated, the trouser break rests on the Derby vamp. SC16: the sheet stays at his waist through the roll, over a skin-matched modesty garment, never seen. SC17: seated upright in the armchair, hands on the arms. SC18: seated upright in PR-19. DOUBLE (SH14 and SH19 plates): he wears SH-2, with SH-3 re-dusted to C3 on standby. Top button fastened, cuffs buttoned, tucked flat, elbow sit creases and the chest fold crease showing, collar points flat at C3 with the shoulders still filmed from C1, dust laid by production design. With it: TR-2, the matched belt on hole 1, black socks, and Derbies in his size built to the PR-41 spec. If SH-2's band doesn't close flat on his neck, wardrobe cuts him his own SH-2 from the same cloth, with the crease and dust states matched.
- **accessories**: PR-04 (set-owned): out of PR-05's outside pocket in SC17 and onto his face. It stays on to V01-B06, only the left arm showing to the phone. Lifted off at V01-B07 and never on him again.; Nothing else worn: no watch, ring or chain; hands bare.; Double: the same bare hands; PR-04 on his face only if a plate serves a beat before V01-B07.
- **hair makeup**: Skin: deep, warm undertone, #6E4631 approx, never lightened by makeup, the grade or the 35mm look. The same hex desaturated, with a grey-ash wash at the lips, the lids and under the eyes. Lips dry and matte, nail beds greyed. Pores, forehead lines and crow's feet show. Eyes (SC16 after the roll, SC18, V01-B07, B08, V02-B04 to B06): open, the upper lid at the top edge of the iris in every shot. Irises dark brown #2A1A12, unchanged. The cornea is dry and dulled with a faint grey film and no wet shine (joint lock with VFX). No dust before V01; from V01-B07, dust #C9A45C sits on the lashes, in the lid creases, the forehead lines and the crow's feet, matching the collar. Hair: black #17110E approx, tight 4A curls, top 12-15 cm stretched and picked out at the crown, so in the wind (B07, V02-B04) the top shifts as one mass and the crown ends flutter. Sides 1 cm, no part, a light dust film at the hairline in V01. Beard 3 mm with the hard cheek line, unchanged. The mole on the left side of the neck stays uncovered. SC16: face-down, mouth open, grey-ash at the lips; after she rolls him, eyes open to the ceiling, then his face set toward her on the pillow. DOUBLE: dense 4A curls in black #17110E approx, top 12-15 cm stretched, picked out at the crown with a wide-tooth pick, light leave-in, no gel, about 5 cm high as it sits; sides and back tapered to 1 cm, no part, no razor line, the left temple receding further. First choice: his own dense 4A hair cut to this. Fallback: a custom human-hair 4A topper covering the top and crown, blended into his own sides clipped to 1 cm and matched to #17110E. Light PR-57 film at the hairline. Neck, ears and hands: deep, warm undertone matched to #6E4631 approx with the same grey-ash, never lightened. If his left neck is in frame, a lentil-sized mole transfer below the ear, 2 cm above the collar band; if his left jaw is in frame, beard at 3 mm with the hard cheek line. Never lighten him or loosen the curl.
- **continuity locks**: [phone frame 0-42 s, V02, S01] Top button fastened: seen from his left side, the collar band runs unbroken to the front (the reverse of JEAN_L01). Collar points lie flat against the shirt; L01's lifted tips are gone. Collar stand clear of the jaw.; [phone frame, V01 0-42 s] Mole on the LEFT side of the neck, 2 cm above the collar band, facing camera. A mole on the right means the frame is mirrored: reject it. Only the left sunglasses arm shows, up to B06.; [phone frame] Collar dust follows PR-57: C1 at B01, C2 at B02, C3 from B05.; [close-up, SC16 after the roll, SC18, V01-B07, B08, V02-B04 to B06] Eyes open, upper lid at the top edge of the iris, no blink, no wet shine; dust on the lashes from V01-B07 only.; [wide] All black, tucked flat with no blouse over the belt, cuffs buttoned, no jewellery.; [medium, from SC17] One sharp horizontal fold crease across the chest, 12 cm below the collar, on top of L01's elbow crumples.; [SC17 held frame in PR-44] No white bouclé fibre on the black shirt or trousers.; [SH14 and SH19 plates, back and crown] The double's top is picked out to about 5 cm as it sits, with 4A curl size and 1 cm sides matching CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1 side by side. SH-2 top button fastened; collar points at C3.

### BARTENDER_L01 (BARTENDER)

- **garment silhouette**: Closet: work clothes bought in multipacks and worn until they give out. He spends on his shoes because he stands for ten hours. Story: Thursday 22 October 2026 (assumed), late shift, SC04-SC08, the bar at about 24 °C: short sleeves, forearms bare. Head to toe: a slate short-sleeve crew-neck T-shirt, tucked in, sleeves to mid-bicep. A tobacco bib apron with a neck strap: bib to the top of the sternum, hem at the knee, ties knotted at the back. Charcoal work trousers, mid rise, plain hem. Dark grey socks. Brown-black slip-resistant leather work shoes. Older than the story: the apron.
- **materials colors**: T-shirt: heavyweight cotton single jersey 220 g/m², solid, matte, slate #56657A (approx L*42, C*14, h 268°). Apron: waxed cotton duck 340 g/m², tobacco #6A4E36 (approx L*35); neck strap and ties sewn on, no hardware. Older than the story: the wax worn off in pale cracks #8A7058 along the waist fold and at the corner of the bib pocket, and the ties hand-darkened for 20 cm either side of the knot. To rebuild, flex and sand the wax. Trousers: cotton twill 280 g/m², charcoal #3D3E40. Socks: cotton, dark grey #4A4B4C. Shoes: leather, brown-black #2E2724, plain rubber sole. Pen: plain black plastic #1F1F21, matte, no print. Bar towel: cotton huckaback, off-white #E6E2D8. No logo, bar name or lettering on any garment, the pen or the towel (A10).
- **fit notes**: 170 cm, US men's XL: barrel chest, shoulders and hips the same width. T-shirt: shoulder seam 2 cm past the shoulder point, 12 cm ease; sleeve hem at mid-bicep, so both forearms are bare above the elbow for the tab-slip set-down and the pours. Tucked. Apron: neck strap at the base of the neck, bib flat to the top of the sternum, ties at the natural waist. From the stools, the 107 cm counter hides everything below the apron waist. Trousers: 2 cm ease, plain hem touching the shoe.
- **accessories**: Hands and forearms bare: no ring, watch or bracelet.; The black pen clipped in the bib pocket.; The off-white bar towel tucked into the apron tie at his right hip.; Set-owned: the PR-34 tab slip and tray.
- **hair makeup**: Skin: medium, warm undertone, #B07B55 approx, never lightened by makeup, the grade or the 35mm look. No base. Left uncovered: the deep forehead lines, the crow's feet, the broken capillaries across the nose, and the pale scar splitting the outer end of the left brow. Head shaved to 1 mm, grey stubble #8E8882 approx at the sides and back. A full grey-black moustache #4A4642 approx with grey through it, trimmed to the corners of the mouth and no further. No beard; jaw shaved. Heavy greying brows, untouched. Do not add hair or de-age him.
- **continuity locks**: [medium] Slate sleeves at mid-bicep, both forearms bare. Tobacco bib apron on, neck strap at the base of the neck.; [close-up] Head shaved to 1 mm with grey stubble at the sides. Moustache to the corners of the mouth. No beard.; [any frame] No lettering or logo on the tee, apron, pen or towel.

### DRIVER_L01 (DRIVER)

- **garment silhouette**: Closet: comfortable, bought once and kept. He spends nothing on clothes and keeps his car cleaner than his shirts. Story: Friday 23 October 2026, 01:47 (PR-36's timestamp, assumed), SC09. The cabin is at about 20 °C with the air on: short sleeves. Head to toe: a sage short-sleeve button-front shirt, point collar, 6 buttons, top button open, tucked in. A white crew undershirt shows at the open neck. Grey-brown twill trousers, mid rise, brown belt. Grey socks. Brown leather walking shoes. Everything below the chest is out of the dash-cam frame. Older than the story: the shirt.
- **materials colors**: Shirt: cotton poplin 115 g/m², solid, sage #7D8A74 (approx L*56, C*14, h 131°), tonal plastic buttons #76826E, matte. Older than the story: the left shoulder and left sleeve sun-faded to #97A28E from the driver's window, collar edges soft. To rebuild, bleach and sand the left side. It is a detail, not an identity mark, because models mirror. Undershirt: cotton single jersey 150 g/m², white #E9E7E1 (approx L*92). Trousers: cotton twill 260 g/m², grey-brown #6E665A. Belt: leather, brown #4A3A2E, buckle in brushed steel #8C8B87 approx, satin. Socks: cotton, grey #6A6A68. Shoes: leather, brown #4A3A2E, plain rubber sole. No rideshare badge, lanyard, logo or lettering (A10).
- **fit notes**: About 180 cm, US men's M: narrow sloping shoulders, thin arms. Shirt: shoulder seam lands 1 cm past the shoulder point because of the slope; 14 cm ease. Sleeve hem at mid-bicep, so his right forearm and hand are bare when they rise into the dome light. Tucked, collar open one button to show the undershirt's crew. He sits upright, both hands on the wheel, his shoulder line a clear slope against the side window.
- **accessories**: Hands and wrists bare: no ring or watch. His right hand rises to the dome switch.; No glasses, lanyard, badge or rideshare marks (A10).; Set-owned: the PR-36 dash cam and the PR-37 dome light.
- **hair makeup**: Skin: light, warm undertone, #DDBBA3 approx, never altered by makeup, the grade or the 35mm look. Left uncovered: the red weathering across the cheeks and nose and the brown age spot at the left temple. No base. Crown scalp left unpowdered, so it shines under the dome light. Hair: thinning silver #CFC9C0 approx, straight, cropped to 1.5 cm, receding high, no part. Clean-shaven. Heavy grey brows, untouched. His right hand keeps its raised veins, age spots and swollen knuckles.
- **continuity locks**: [dash-cam frame] Sage shirt, top button open, white undershirt crew showing. Right sleeve at mid-bicep, forearm bare.; [dash-cam frame] Silver crop receding high, crown scalp showing. Clean-shaven. No glasses.; [dash-cam frame] No badge, lanyard or lettering anywhere on him.

## Director's treatment

### SC01

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat B07 (42.0-50.0): close on her right hand turning his chin. His open eyes are the one face shot (taste note: hands before faces). She wants him to look back at her. Two things stop her: he is dead, and she won't turn the lens on herself. B01 (0-4), to wake him to the drive: she laughs loud on the mic, the way you laugh at a lover who sleeps through the best part, and the frame jolts. B02 (4-10), to make him presentable: a full stop at a sign nobody else will ever reach. Real detail: she obeys a stop on an empty road. Her left hand brushes the dust off him, flattens his collar, rests a beat and pulls away. B03 (10-18) is a hold: three jumps and the pose never changes, which is the joke. B04 (18-26), to get into the picture: her right thumb rests 2.2 s on the flip icon, played as shyness, then slides off, and she laughs. B05 (26-34), to keep seeing him as the light goes: the video light comes on. B06 (34-42) shows her face for the first time. Her head is turned about 45° to him and she laughs with her eyes on him, never on the road or any lens. No knowing look, no lip bite. B07: the dark is her excuse to take off his sunglasses. She draws them forward off his face from the near side and never reaches round him. Then she turns his face toward her own seat, and her laugh stops. Her fingers stay on his chin 1.2 s after his head stops. Play no shock, because she already knows. B08 (50-56), back behind the lens: she hooks his sunglasses at her own neckline and laughs more softly. His eyes now aim just past the lens toward her. Her thumb rests only 1.2 s: 'not like this, not yet'. JEAN, the head-alone rule, LOCKED for all of V01, S01 and D01's dead beats: his head moves only when her hand moves it. It turns alone on the neck with torso and shoulders square, stays where she leaves it, and never lolls or bobs with the car, a bump or her laugh. No blink, no breath. CONFIRMED: his right forearm lies slack along the passenger door top and never moves (SC01-SC02, S01).
- **blocking**: LOC-01, top down, in LOC-02. The car heads north in the northbound lane, with the low west sun behind the driver's side (wall A). VENUS sits in the driver seat (y 32), left hand on the worn 10 o'clock arc. PR-01 is upright in her right hand at arm's length, wrist on the PR-57 arc (x 20-30, y 45-65). The lens aims back across Jean's left shoulder toward his door, so PR-11 and PR-10 pass behind his head. JEAN is in P1: torso square in the seat, facing north. His head is turned 90° right on the neck, right cheek on the pad (x 108, y 100), face to his door (car-frame 0°, compass 90°). PR-04 is on, with its right arm trapped between his temple and the pad. His right forearm lies slack along the door top (72 cm), the hand over the outer edge. The phone sees only his collar, left jaw, left ear, left sunglasses arm and neck mole. Wind is relative, nose to tail. B02: the front bumper stops at x 3,096 with PR-58 just ahead. PR-10 sits about 7° to the door side of his head and 11° above it, facing the lens. Line of action: the road, always north. Every full-width setup stays on or west of the car's centerline. From B06 to 45.0 the camera is ahead of the car, about 10° west of her straight-ahead line, looking back south. In B06 she reads as a deep three-quarter, laughing at him, and Jean is a lost profile: the back of his head and the left sunglasses arm. 41.6-45.0 is a medium two-shot, both of them waist up. Her right hand crosses in front of his face on the windshield side, takes PR-04's left arm at the hinge and draws the frame north along the pad toward the windshield. The right arm slides out from under his cheek, and the lift travels toward the lens. At 45.0 the camera is on the front axis, on or just west of the centerline, and the size steps in to a close shot of his head and her two fingers, his face about half the frame height. Her two fingers hook the windshield side of his chin and turn his head alone 120° on the neck, through dead ahead, to P2: car-frame 240° (compass 330°), 30° past dead ahead toward her seat. From this axis his gaze passes the lens on its west side, toward her, and he reads as a three-quarter with both eyes. P2 holds through B08 and SC02, and at the SC18 table it becomes 30° to his left. B08: PR-01 is back on the wrist arc at about y 60, a hand's width (8 cm) to the passenger side of his gaze line, so his eyes read frontal and centered, aimed about 5° past the lens toward her. 9:16: the phone frames are native upright. In B07 her hand and his face stay inside the middle third on both axes, because V02-B03 uses 42.8-43.8 and 45.0-46.0.
- **action progression**: Entry at 0.0: PR-01 recording, light off; collar C1; dash D2; PR-04 on; PR-61 key 1 in the ignition. B01, 0.0-4.0, one plate: hold on collar and jaw (1.8); a full-torso laugh jolts the frame (1.6); settle (0.6). B02, 4.0-10.0: she rolls to a full stop and the frame dips (0.8); her left hand leaves the wheel and enters under the lens (0.7); two strokes take the collar from C1 to C2 (1.2); she pinches the left point flat (1.0); the hand rests (0.8); hidden cut at 8.9 as the withdrawing hand wipes the lens (0.4); the frame lurches away over PR-58 (1.1). Plates: 4.0-8.9 (SH02) and 8.9-10.0. B03, 10.0-18.0, three plates: 10.0-12.6 storefronts (the second pass), 12.6-15.3 open desert, 15.3-18.0 scrub hills. Each cut lands on a snatch of laugh while the dust builds toward C3. B04, 18.0-26.0: hold (0.8); right thumb onto the icon (1.0); rests (2.2); slides to the frame edge (0.8); laugh jolt at 22.8, with the plate join at 22.9 hidden in its blur; laugh (2.0); the frame settles on him (1.2). B05, 26.0-34.0, REC 01:04:29: noisy dusk picture (2.7); side-button press off picture (0.3); at 29.0 the light comes on, the join hidden in the exposure pop or SH10's road bump; C3 glints (5.0). The phone records unbroken from 26.0 to 52.0. B06, 34.0-42.0: phone plate 34.0-35.4, joined inside a laugh jolt, shows REC 01:04:37 for 0.8 s (19 frames); the view tilts toward her lap under the opening pillars (0.6). Ahead-west plates 34.8-38.2 and 38.2-41.6, joined in a laugh jolt: the phone is face up in her lap, still recording, light on; her right hand rests on her thigh; her head is turned 45° to him, laughing. B07, 42.0-50.0. Plate 41.6-45.0, medium two-shot, joined in the last laugh jolt (0.4): her right hand goes from her thigh to him (0.8); it takes PR-04's left arm at the hinge and draws the frame forward along the pad toward the windshield, the right arm sliding out from under his cheek while his head holds against the pad (1.6); it drops to jaw height, the frame hanging open by its left arm from her thumb and ring finger, index and middle fingers free (0.6). Cut on action at 45.0, stepping in to the close, as two fingers meet his chin. Plate 45.0-50.0: his head alone turns 120° to P2, torso still, collar dragged only at the jaw, with no overshoot and no loll (1.8); her fingers stay (1.2); they withdraw out of frame (0.4); hold on the open eyes, hair moving, no blink (1.6). Her left hand stays on the wheel throughout. B08, 50.0-56.0. Hard cut at 50.0 to plate 50.0-52.0 (SH16), a front-axis two-shot: her right hand hooks PR-04's open left arm into her neckline, lenses out (1.0); lifts PR-01 from her lap (0.6); the frame closes to upright (0.4). Plate 52.0-56.0 (SH17), phone frame, REC 01:04:55: his eyes centered under the light (hold); laugh shake (1.0); thumb to the icon (0.8); rests (1.2); slides back (0.6); hold (0.4). Generate this plate long for V02.
- **emotional shift**: Hers. Before (B01-B06): laughing and filming, eyes on him. Trigger (B07), seen by the camera: she turns his face toward her seat, and his open eyes land facing her side and still miss her face. After: no laugh for 8 s, and her fingers stay on his chin 1.2 s. In B08 she takes his sunglasses for herself and goes back behind the lens. The laugh returns softer and the thumb rest is shorter (1.2 s against 2.2 s), so the same gesture now reads 'not like this, not yet'. B04 is a hold: no trigger, the same state, shyness. Up to B07, the viewer's shift from lover to dead man comes from his stillness, not from any change in her.
- **transition in**: The pilot opens with no fade: from black straight into the phone's upright frame at REC 00:00:04. Her laugh and the wind on the mic start on frame one.
- **transition out**: Hard cut with a time jump to last light: from B08's phone frame (thumb leaving the icon, wind on the mic) to B09's full-width carport on 35mm. The mic wind cuts dead and the cooling engine's ping starts on the cut. The eye goes from his eyes at the center of the upright frame to the car under the MOTEL sign, also at center.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' B04 and B08 carry it: twice her right thumb rests on the flip-camera icon over him, then slides back. Cut this and the viewer loses the want: a woman who films a man for an hour and can't turn the lens around. The wall, the table for two and SC18's press would then read as a collector's trophies.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Head-alone rule, LOCKED. Method: G, with one locked head plate reused wherever the pose holds. Acceptance: in B01-B06 his head holds P1 in every plate, through every laugh, jolt and bump. In B07 it moves only during the 1.8 s turn, never during the glasses lift, and turns alone on the neck with torso and shoulders unmoved; the collar shifts only where the jaw drags it. From 46.8 it holds P2 through B08 and SC02. Risk: a bob reads alive, a loll reads as a rag doll, and a shoulder turn breaks SC02 and SC18.; Glasses lift, B07 42.8-44.4. Method: G on the locked head plate; if the trapped right arm drags his head, composite PR-04 as a CG element drawn off by her hand plate. Acceptance: his head holds against the pad, and the only movement is the lenses clearing his face; the right arm slides out from under his cheek without lifting it. Risk: the head follows the glasses and reads alive, which breaks the head rule.; The 120° turn, from P1 (90° right) to P2 (30° left). Acceptance: P1 reads as a sleeping head resting on the pad, not a wrenched neck, and the turn passes dead ahead with no overshoot or bounce. Risk: an extreme P1 reads as a broken neck. Fallback: ease P1 to about 80° right, with the cheek still on the pad.; Cut at 45.0. Acceptance: both setups are ahead of the car and neither is east of the centerline. The size steps from a medium two-shot to a close shot of his head (face about half the frame height), so the cut reads as a cut in and not as a jump cut, since the axis moves only about 13°. His gaze passes the lens at least 10° on its west side. The lens choice is the DP's. Risk: an east setup puts his gaze into the lens.; Right forearm, confirmed. Acceptance: it lies slack along the passenger door top, hand over the outer edge, in every plate of B01-B11 and in S01. It never moves, including under her lean in B09, and never enters the phone frame.; Phone UI (REC dot, timer, flip icon). Method: composited in finishing; an original design, clearance pending (A10). Acceptance: the timer reads 00:00:04, 00:06:12, 00:21:40, 00:38:15, 00:52:30, 00:53:10, 01:04:29, 01:04:37 (19 frames) and 01:04:55. It never jumps inside the 26.0-52.0 take, and there are no OS or maker marks.; Plate map, 5 s rule. Acceptance: no generated plate runs over 5.0 s, and every join lands on its named cover: 8.9, 22.9, 29.0, 34.0, 38.2, 41.6, 45.0 and 52.0. 50.0 is a hard cut between beats.; Camera-plan ruling, SH02 sign cheat: APPROVED. PR-10 is now spun on its post to face 325° in the world bible, so the cheat belongs to the world. Composite 'STOP' if the letters distort.; Camera-plan ruling, SH10 road-bump join: APPROVED. Acceptance: the bump moves the frame and the car body, with a tire thump on the mic. Jean's head, collar and sunglasses hold within a few pixels relative to the headrest pad (head rule).; Thumb on the icon (B04, B08; V02-B06). Method: a practical plate of the hand double's right thumb (the 6 mm nick) over a blank phone on green, composited over G. Acceptance: it enters nail-side, rests 2.2 s or 1.2 s without a twitch and never presses, so the UI never changes before SC18. Fallback: the script's under-a-second flip, with her eyes on him.; Face reveal, B06. Acceptance: she reads as a deep three-quarter with both eyes, laughing at him, while Jean reads as a lost profile. Risk: the back of her head at the route's first look at her face.; B08 eyes. Acceptance: his face is frontal and centered, and his eyes aim about 5° past the lens toward her, never into it.; Dead eyes (B07-B08; V02-B04/B05). Method: G, locked in composite. Acceptance, tested after degrain: zero blinks and zero eye movement across 45.0-50.0 and 52.0-56.0; lids at the top edge of the iris; dry, dulled corneas; dust on the lashes; hair moving. Fallback: freeze the eye region from the best frame and regrain it with no seam at 100%. A16: first try in the moving car.; Collar touch, B02 (V02-B01). Method: G; the hand is a risk. Acceptance: one hand, two strokes, collar C1 to C2, and his head doesn't move. Fallback: her hand rests on his collar.; Three-step hand chains, B07 and B08. Acceptance: PR-04 hangs by its left arm from her thumb and ring finger across the 45.0 cut and is hooked, lenses out, by 51.0; it never vanishes or doubles. Fallback: already hooked at 50.0.; Vehicle. Method: G. Acceptance: one open 1950s-shape two-seater per LOC-01: left-hand drive, blank rear plate, no badges, no numerals. No other vehicle, person or animal in any frame (A10).; B03 jump cuts. Acceptance: one locked G of Jean over the background plates; head, collar and sunglasses overlay within a few pixels.; Laugh sync, B06 only. Acceptance: laugh peaks land within 2 frames of the torso shakes. One laugh voice across V01, V02 and D01.; Hair in wind: hers is held by the stylist and never crosses the hand shots. His top hair moves as one mass in B07.; No slow motion; everything plays at 24 fps real speed (taste note).; Frame open and close (B06, B08): built in finishing on the phone's own motion. The pillars open in 0.6 s and close in 0.4 s, and the texture switches at the same moment. No dissolve.; Left-index burn: KEEP. A 1 cm glossy oval on the back of the left index finger, applied as makeup on the hand double. Acceptance: present in every frame where the back of her left hand reads (B02, the wheel shots, D01). Fallback: paint it in if a generated plate drops it.; S01 comes from this scene's gold light before B04, shot from behind her right shoulder. Jean is in P1 with his forearm on the door top; the phone shows him under the REC dot; she laughs in three-quarter profile with her eyes on him. This breaks 'hands before faces' on purpose, because a poster needs its lead.

### SC02

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat B10: close on the unbroken dust as she leans over the office step to tug the padlock rather than set a foot on it. She wants proof that nobody has found the motel, and she won't bring him in until she has it. B09 (56-60), to arm herself: she leans across his knees to the glovebox, and her face passes through his open gaze about 50 cm from his eyes. She doesn't look away (casting). She takes the pistol by the grip with the muzzle toward the dash, never over his legs, and seats it grip-up in her right back pocket. Real detail: trained handling, even beside a dead man. B10 (60-64), to check: she walks the frontage alone, glances once at the empty highway, tugs the padlock twice and looks down at the dust. B11 (64-72), to move in: one fast trip with both shoulders loaded and no look back at him, then she shoulders the swollen door. JEAN: P2, still, face turned toward her empty seat. Her lean doesn't move him.
- **blocking**: LOC-03, last light. Wind comes from the highway (south), and the fronds lean toward the room block. The car is nosed in facing north at x 21.0-22.55, y 13.5-17.5. Its passenger side is toward PR-15 (x 25.7-26.6), and the trunk is 6 m from it. Jean is in P2 (compass 330°): his gaze crosses the driver's seat to room 2's frontage (about x 20.0). Entry, set across the cut: VENUS in the driver seat, engine off. PR-01 is in her right front jeans pocket and PR-61 in her left; PR-04 hangs at her neckline. PR-02 is in the unlocked glovebox (y 76-106), muzzle toward his door. B09: she leans east across his knees, and her head crosses his gaze line about 50 cm in front of his face. The lid drops and stays open. B10: getting out is elided. She walks south-west about 10 m to the office door (x 12, south face) and stops on the gravel, toes off PR-14. The way back is not seen. B11: from the trunk north-east along her worn track to PR-15, on the side his face is turned from. She shoulders the rubbed patch (143-173 cm); the door sticks at 35°, then opens to 90°. Both bags drop just inside the latch-side jamb (LOC-04, x 195-245, y 10-50).
- **action progression**: B09, 56.0-60.0, one plate (SH18): engine ping (0.4); she leans across his knees, right hand to the glovebox (0.9); thumbs the latch and the lid drops (0.4); lifts PR-02 by the grip, muzzle toward the dash (0.7); sits back upright (0.8); lifts her right hip and seats it grip-up in her right back pocket, 6 cm showing (0.8). Both hands end free. Hard cut. B10, 60.0-64.0, one plate (SH19): the last 2.3 m of her walk, grip showing, one glance south at the highway (1.8); she stops at the step's edge (0.5); leans and tugs PR-13 twice with her right hand, and it knocks and stays locked (0.7); straightens and looks down at the unbroken dust (1.0). Hard cut. B11, 64.0-72.0. Plate A (SH20), 64.0-68.0, at the trunk: lid up (0.8); PR-05 onto her left shoulder (1.1); PR-06 onto her right (1.0); both PR-07 sacks hugged to her chest (1.1). The trunk is left open; Jean is in shot, the back of his head to camera. Hard cut. Plate B (SH21), 68.0-72.0, at PR-15: she arrives laden (0.4); turns her right shoulder to the patch (0.4); first shove, and the door sticks at 35° (0.7); second shove, and it scrapes to 90° (1.0); she steps in (0.7); shrugs both bags off at the latch-side jamb (0.8). The sacks stay at her chest.
- **emotional shift**: Before: quick and armed, with one glance at the highway during the walk. Trigger, seen by the camera: the unbroken dust on PR-14. After: she turns back without another look at the road and unloads at speed. She checks nothing else for the rest of the cold open.
- **transition in**: Hard cut with a time jump from SC01's phone frame to the full-width carport on 35mm. The engine ping replaces the mic wind.
- **transition out**: Hard cut on the door scraping open. Her back goes into the dark doorway at frame center, and we cut to the black room inside, where the lantern click lights the wall (SC03). The scrape carries across the cut.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' B11 carries it: she carries his bag beside hers into the room, moving in with him rather than stashing a body. Cut this and the viewer loses that the motel is a home she has kept and checked for the two of them. Without the dust check and the pistol guarding it, the room reads as a trophy lair instead of a house for two.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Pistol PR-02, B09-B10. Method: G. If a practical plate is needed: a non-firing replica under an armorer who checks it before each take and holds it between takes (producer; pending). Acceptance: the muzzle never crosses Jean, crew or the lens; grip up, 6 cm showing against light-wash denim; no markings. Fallback: already in the pocket, no hand on it.; Camera-plan ruling, SH18 vertical jolt: APPROVED only as the car rocking on its springs under her lean. The car and Jean move as one, his head never moves relative to his shoulders, and the jolt never lands during the pistol lift.; Camera-plan ruling, SH20 on the axis: APPROVED. The camera stays south of the car on its centerline, Jean shows only the back of his head, and the open trunk lid never hides the count of two bags and two sacks.; Leaning across the body, B09, a five-step chain. Acceptance: his knees, forearm and head don't move. Risk: he shifts and reads alive.; PR-14. Acceptance: no footprint on the step in any frame of B10 or later.; Carry, B11. Acceptance: exactly two bags and two sacks; nothing appears or vanishes across the 68.0 cut.; PR-12: 'MOTEL' and 'OFFICE' verbatim, composited if garbled; no other text. The loose panel may tick.; Door, B11. Acceptance: two shoves with a stop at 35°, not one swing.

### SC03

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her hands and the chair legs as she drags the second chair up and squares it beside hers. She wants the table set for two. Play her hands. Her head stays above the lantern's pool and never turns to the door (taste note: hands before faces). She unpacks fast, in pairs and handfuls, like someone who has done it many times. When the last jug lands she gives one look, straight across the table at the bare boards where his chair will go. She slows only for the chair, then leaves to bring him in and drags the swollen door shut behind her. The held frame has nobody in it.
- **blocking**: LOC-04. The held-frame view is from the door end facing wall C; exact placement is the DP's. PR-15 is open at 90°. PR-05 and PR-06 lie at the latch-side jamb (x 195-245, y 10-50), below the held frame. PR-16 is on the table, off, at x 180, y 365. She works standing at the table's south edge around x 190, y 305, facing north, with everything in reach. She reads in silhouette against the lit wall, and wall C stays clear above her and on either side. Placements: PR-25 at x 145, y 340, slot south; PR-21 at x 182-198, y 382-397; PR-24 at x 195-225, y 340-360; PR-22 at x 206-238, y 382-397. Her one look goes to the bare boards at x 210, y 420, beside PR-18. PR-19 travels from wall D (x 315-360, y 280-325) round the table's north-east corner to x 210, y 420, facing south. Exit: south past the table's east end, clear of the bags at the jamb, out through PR-15; from outside she drags it shut (PR-15 S3, pending PD). The bare pine at x 280-345 stays empty.
- **action progression**: Entry at 72.0: dark room, sacks against her chest. B12, 72.0-82.0. Plate A (SH22), 72.0-74.9: sacks onto the table's south edge (0.6); her right hand clicks the lantern slider and the wall appears (0.5); she lifts the printer off sack 1 and sets it square, slot to the door (0.9); first pair of cans down (0.9). Hidden cut at 74.9 as her arm crosses the lantern. Plate B (SH23), 74.9-78.5: four cans already stacked, the last pair going on (0.8); the oranges were placed in the cut; the two full jugs come out one per hand and land side by side (1.2); her head lifts to the bare boards across the table and holds (0.9); head down, and her hands slide the emptied sacks off the edge (0.7). Cut into the action at 78.5 as she turns east; the walk to wall D and folding the sacks under PR-17 are elided. Plate C (SH24), 78.5-82.0: PR-19 dragged by its back over the last 0.8 m round the north-east corner (1.0); both hands square it beside PR-18 (0.7); one nudge levels the seat edges (0.3); both hands rest on its back (0.7); she walks south out of frame toward the door, clear by 82.0 (0.8). Hard cut at 82.0. B13, 82.0-88.0 (SH25): a locked hold in which nothing moves. Behind camera, her steps cross the boards (82.0-84.2), then the swollen door scrapes and thuds as she drags it shut from outside (84.2-85.2). From 85.2 there is only the wind pushing at the shut door. B14, 88.0-90.0: the same frame; the LIMERENCE card lands and the wind cuts to silence at 88.0.
- **emotional shift**: Before: fast unpacking, hands never pausing. Trigger, seen by the camera: the last jug lands and her head lifts to the bare boards across the table. After: her hands slow. The chair gets 2.7 s against 1.7 s for six cans, with two hands, one nudge and both hands resting 0.7 s on its back, the only rest in the scene. B13-B14 are a hold: the room carries it.
- **transition in**: Hard cut from SC02's doorway into the dark room. The door scrape carries across, and the lantern click reveals the wall.
- **transition out**: In V01: the LIMERENCE card sits on the held frame in silence, then cut to black at 90.0. In the pilot: a hard cut from the card's silence to SC04's upright phone frame, whose first frame echoes V01-B01's collar and jaw, with Jean alive.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-20 over the table carries it, with PR-19 pulled up beside PR-18: a wall of him alone, none of them together, above a table set for two. Cut this and the viewer loses the gap the idea lives in, and the printer (PR-25) that SC18 pays off.
- **practical or compositing flags**: PR-20 wall (verified fact: none of them together). Method: composite about 40 unique long-lens images of the locked JEAN, tiled to 250 prints on the world bible's grid as a CG texture on the pine. Acceptance at S02 resolution: no print contains a second person; no legible text; Jean in black in every print; the pine at x 280-345 is bare. Risk: a freely generated wall invents couple photos.; Camera-plan ruling, SH22 at the left third: APPROVED. It keeps the bare pine and PR-19's destination clear.; Camera-plan ruling, SH23's head above frame: RETURNED. The head lift at 76.9-77.8 is the scene's only trigger. Smallest fix: her head, in silhouette, is in frame from 76.9 to 77.8 and seen holding on the bare boards; above frame before 76.9 is fine.; Camera-plan ruling, SH24 at 325°: APPROVED. Acceptance: PR-19 ends square beside PR-18 in frame, and her exit clears frame by 82.0.; Camera-plan ruling, the SC03 hard cut at 82.0: APPROVED in place of my body-wipe hidden cut. Acceptance: SH25 is empty from its first frame, and the chair, cans and jugs match SH24.; Door dragged shut, B13. Acceptance: her steps and the scrape and thud are heard behind camera at 82.0-85.2, and nothing in frame moves with them; from 85.2 the wind knocks a shut door in its latch, with no draft in the room. It can't land before the 82.0 cut without cutting the chair's 2.7 s or the head lift; the sound-line change is filed with the writer.; Held frame, B13-B14 and S02. Method: lock the content; the lantern runs on batteries, so nothing flickers. Acceptance, tested after degrain: no motion in props, light, dust or photos across 82.0-90.0, then live grain and gate weave back over the whole frame.; Unpacking across the 74.9 cut. Acceptance: the counts hold (6 cans, 2 full jugs, 5 oranges, 1 printer, 9 empty jugs); nothing lands without a hand or the cut placing it; nothing is sped up.; Title: LIMERENCE only, final 2 s, silent, composited. S02 is the B13 frame with no text.

### V02

- **performance beats**: No new performance: V02 is rebuilt from SC01's plates. It stays Venus's piece, seen through her phone. Hero beat V02-B06: her thumb rests on the flip icon over his open eyes, then slides back. It has to read with the sound off.
- **blocking**: Native 9:16. The phone-frame plates (B01, B02, B04-B06) are the upright frame at full height, with nothing restaged, generated at 1080x1920 or better. B03 is cut from V01-B07's two setups: 42.8-43.8, the medium two-shot as the frame is drawn forward off his face, toward the lens, and 45.0-46.0, the close on his head passing dead ahead. Her hand and his face sit in the middle third of both, and the lift travels toward the lens, not across the frame, so the crop holds it. B04 is a static punch-in on the B08 eyes plate: no push.
- **action progression**: B01, 0.0-1.5: V01 5.5-7.0, the brush and the collar, REC 00:06:13. B02, 1.5-4.0: V01 15.3-17.8, scrub hills, REC 00:52:30. B03, 4.0-6.0: V01 42.8-43.8, then a jump to 45.0-46.0; 35mm texture, no UI. B04, 6.0-9.0: a handle from the B08 eyes plate, punched in so his eyes fill the frame from its first frame, and still; REC 01:04:55. B05, 9.0-11.0: the laugh shake, REC 01:04:58. B06, 11.0-13.0 (V02-SH07), REC 01:05:00: the thumb enters nail-side on f0 and lands on the icon (0.3); rests over his eyes (1.2); slides back (0.4); the mic cuts (0.1). B07, 13.0-15.0: black, the REC dot gone, LIMERENCE centered, silent.
- **emotional shift**: The same shift as SC01, compressed. Before: care (the collar) and time (the timer jump). Trigger: his open eyes under the light (B04). After: her laugh shakes the frame while his eyes stay still (B05), and her thumb rests and retreats (B06). With the sound off, the timer and the unblinking eyes carry time and death, and the thumb carries the want.
- **transition in**: Opens straight on the phone frame mid-action at the collar, with wind on the mic from frame one.
- **transition out**: The recording stops: hard cut to black at 13.0 with the mic wind cut dead, then LIMERENCE in silence to 15.0.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' B06 carries it: her thumb on the flip icon over his open eyes, sliding back. Cut it and V02 becomes a dead-body reveal; the want, and the reason to open V01, are gone.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Camera-plan ruling, no push in V02-B04: APPROVED. B04 is a static punch-in. Acceptance: at the punched size the dull corneas and the dust on the lashes hold, so generate the eyes plate at 4K equivalent. If the eye-freeze fallback is used, it passes the no-seam test at that size.; Camera-plan ruling, V02-SH07 thumb entering on f0: APPROVED. Acceptance: the thumb is moving on B06's first frame, rests 1.2 s without a twitch, and never presses.; Footage length. V01-B08's phone section runs 4.0 s, and B04-B06 need about 7 s on his eyes. Method: generate the eyes plate with handles (two plates of up to 5 s joined on a laugh jolt) and shoot the thumb plate long. Acceptance: no frame used twice.; B03 crop. Acceptance: in both source windows her hand, the glasses and his face stay inside the middle third; the lift reads as the frame coming off his face toward the lens.; Teaser clock. Acceptance: the timer runs continuously within each beat on the script's teaser values, deliberately not frame-matched to V01.; Muted read. Acceptance: with the sound off, a test viewer reads 'a man who never wakes, and she won't turn the camera on herself'. The REC timer and the title are the only text.

### SC04

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her hands as the pistol goes into the glovebox and the tin comes out. She wants to go in before he leaves alone again, and walking into his frame means going without the gun. Tactic 1: starting from V01-B01's collar-and-jaw framing, she zooms toward his face until the lens runs out and the picture breaks into grain, and she keeps it there. Real detail: she'd rather have his face soft than the room sharp. Tactic 2: she waits while he raises two fingers for another. Trigger: the BARTENDER sets the tab slip face down in front of him, and she moves at once, tidying as she goes. Nothing is left on the seat, the glovebox is locked, the curtain goes on. Jean's living face stays soft and grainy inside her phone, a picture rather than a performance shot.
- **blocking**: LOC-01, top up. The passenger curtain is fitted; the driver's curtain is off, stowed behind her seat. The car is parked at the far curb, driver's side to the bar at 14 m. VENUS in the driver seat: PR-01 upright in her right hand at the open side; PR-02 grip-up in her right back pocket; PR-61 key 1 in the ignition. PR-08 (band on) lies on the passenger cushion with PR-09 half under it; PR-03 is closed in the glovebox. Through the window (LOC-05 wall A, x 40-440): JEAN on stool 2 (y 350), PR-04 hooked in his placket, PR-33 at x 148, y 395, PR-05 under the stool; the BARTENDER in the aisle. She gets out of the driver door (street side, facing the bar) and crosses to the glazed door (x 470-560).
- **action progression**: Opens inside her upright phone frame on his black collar and jaw through the glass, alive and lifting his glass (2.0). She zooms until his face breaks into grain (4.0) and holds (to about 0:40). A 1.5 s insert of PR-09. He raises two fingers and is poured another (to about 1:00). The BARTENDER sets PR-34 face down at x 150, y 360 (1.0). The trade: PR-01 into her right chest pocket as the frame opens to full width, phone to 35mm (0.8); her right hand draws PR-02, hip lifted, muzzle down (1.2); she leans right and her left hand drops the lid (0.8); her right hand lays PR-02 in, muzzle toward the passenger door (0.8); takes PR-03 (0.6) into the jacket's inside left pocket, crossing under the lapel (1.0). Both hands sweep PR-08 and PR-09 in on top of the gun, the report folding once (1.5). Her left hand shuts the lid (0.4). Her right hand pulls the ring from the ignition (0.5) and turns key 2 in the glovebox knob (0.8); her left hand pockets the ring in her left front jeans pocket (0.8). Her right hand lifts the driver's curtain from behind the seat (0.8), seats its pegs in the door top and snaps it (1.8). Out of the door (2.0) and across the street (4.0), hands empty.
- **emotional shift**: Before: still, phone up, zoomed in, barely blinking. Trigger: the tab slip lands in her frame. After: she lowers the phone for the first time and her hands move fast. It is the only hurry in the pilot until SC17's scrub.
- **transition in**: Hard cut from V01's silent card. The first frame repeats V01-B01's framing (upright phone frame, REC dot, black collar and jaw) with him alive. Muffled bar noise and street sound come in on the cut.
- **transition out**: Cut on action: her hand hits the bar door's glass from outside, and SC05 opens inside as the door swings in, with Jean's back in the foreground. Street sound becomes bar room tone.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-08 and the lowered phone carry it: months of him alone, all shot from behind her lens, and then she puts the phone away and walks into his frame. Cut this and the viewer loses where the wall came from and the first time she steps out from behind the camera toward him.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Her phone screen. Method: a practical plate of Jean through real bar glass, with the UI and grainy zoom composited. Acceptance: the UI matches V01 exactly, and Jean stays readable through the reflections at the end of the zoom (soft is fine, lost is not).; PR-09. Acceptance: only 'NO CHARGES FILED' is legible, in one 1.5 s insert; no agency, seal or case number; fictional (A02).; Pistol, tin and key chain. Method: practical, a non-firing replica under an armorer who checks it before each take and holds it between takes (producer; pending). Acceptance: the muzzle never crosses her, crew or the lens, with no crew or camera on the passenger side for the lay-in. The tin is never opened. The report folds and nothing is left on the cushion.; PR-08. Acceptance: no text, plates or storefront names in any print that reads.; Street. Acceptance: no legible brand, plate or sign (A10).

### SC05

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on the straw as her fingertip lays the first one in the wet ring his glass left. She wants him to agree to her game; JEAN wants to finish his drink without being seen. 'Have we met before?' is a test she already knows the answer to. Play it straight: she is listening for a lie. He tells the truth, and she gives nothing away. Her phone stays in her right chest pocket all scene and her hand never goes to it. It is the first time she sits in his frame with no lens between them. The Naked and Famous joke lands with her right hand on his forearm and a real laugh, and we cut on the laugh. JEAN starts rounded over his glass, elbow on the worn patch. The mimosa jab gets to him: he props himself up and turns his stool to her. BARTENDER: eyes on the tab, 'What can I get you guys?' said half to the counter. Real detail: Jean slides the tab slip back unsigned with one finger, without looking at it.
- **blocking**: LOC-05. VENUS comes in by the glazed door (wall A, x 470-560) and walks north behind the stools, behind Jean's back, to stool 3 (y 440). She turns the stool fully to him and keeps her elbows off the counter. JEAN is on stool 2 (y 350) with PR-05 underneath. PR-29 stands at x 130, y 480 by her elbow. Jean lifts PR-33 off x 148, y 395 and sets it at x 140, y 320, away from her, leaving the wet ring between them. She lays the base straw through the ring at x 148, y 385-405. PR-32 lands at x 140, y 350 (his) and x 140, y 440 (hers). PR-34 goes to x 105, y 360 and is taken.
- **action progression**: Door to stool (5.0); she sits and turns the stool (1.5). Dialogue to 'Sure.' Jean drinks and moves his glass away (2.0). Her right hand draws a straw from the caddy (0.8) and lays it flat in the ring (1.0). On the rules her fingertip traces the house outline on the bare counter. Then his stall, the bartender and her order. Her right hand passes her pocket and lands on his forearm on 'naked' (0.5), stays through 'Kidding' and lifts on the laugh (1.0). He props up and turns his stool (2.0), then slides PR-34 back unsigned (1.0). The coupes are set down (3.0) and touch on 'Cheers' (1.0).
- **emotional shift**: Jean's. Before: rounded shoulders, eyes on his glass. Trigger: her hand on his forearm and the mimosa jab. After: he sits up, turns his stool to her and pushes the tab back unsigned. Venus holds the same warm attention; nothing has been tested yet.
- **transition in**: Cut on action from SC04: the door swinging in.
- **transition out**: Hard cut on the clink of the coupes, which rings over into SC06's first question.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-01 carries it: its bare black top edge shows at her chest pocket all scene, and her right hand goes to the straws and his forearm, never to it. Cut it and the viewer loses her first minutes in his picture without her camera, the step SC18's front camera completes.
- **practical or compositing flags**: PR-01 in her pocket. Acceptance: its top edge reads as bare black glass in at least one two-shot, and her hand never touches it (stylist confirms the pocket).; Wet ring and straw, practical. Acceptance: the base straw lies inside a visible 8 cm wet ring, white on #3A2418.; 'Naked and Famous': name clearance pending with the producer (A10); no legible bottle label.; Background patrons are low-contrast and never cross the straw house or the candle.; Hand on forearm: practical. Drinks are set down and the slip is slid; nothing is handed over.

### SC06

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her fingertip, face unchanged, as she drinks and draws the wall straw back out. She wants the truth about his ex; JEAN wants to stay the wronged man. 'I was wondering when you'd ask' is a private joke, since she has known his name for months, and she lets it pass as flirting. She lays the wall straw on 'a stick toward our home'. 'Who slayed you, my friend?' is the trial question, played light, leaning in, laughing. His lie comes out easy, with no tell. On 'Packed while I was at work' her eyes drop once to the packed duffel under his stool and come back to him. That is the verdict, with no voiceover. Real detail: he denies something nobody asked. 'That's a drink.' lands at the same pitch as her jokes. Then she moves closer, not away: 'Are you willing to save me?', and pulls back laughing.
- **blocking**: Same marks as SC05. PR-05 is under stool 2 at x 175-225, y 336-364, its zip 20 cm short of closing. Straw house S1 to S2: she lays the wall on his side, x 128-148 at y 385, making an L. S3: she draws it back out and drops it in the caddy (x 130, y 480). Her coupe goes to two-thirds. On 'save me' she slides her stool until the gap is gone, leans to his right ear, then sits back.
- **action progression**: Names (dialogue). She lays the wall straw (1.0). The 'defeated' exchange and her laugh; cut on the laugh. The lie: her eyes go to the duffel and back (1.2). The verdict: she lifts her coupe, takes one swallow and sets it down (2.0); her fingertip draws the wall straw out along the counter (1.0) and drops it in the caddy (0.5). 'That's a no?' 'That's a drink.' Stool slide (1.0); lean to his ear (1.0); the line; she pulls back laughing (1.0); 'My turn.'
- **emotional shift**: Hers. Before: laughing, leaning in. Trigger: 'I never once raised my voice', a denial nobody asked for, said over his packed bag. After: she drinks without taking her eyes off him, takes the straw with no change of face, then leans closer. His: he hears it as flirting and leans in.
- **transition in**: The clink from SC05 bridges the cut.
- **transition out**: Cut on action, continuous: on 'My turn' his left hand closes on PR-31's glass at x 115, y 260, 90 cm to his left, and SC07 picks up the draw in the same instant. The eye goes from her face pulling back, laughing, to his hand at the flame.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' The wall straw coming back out carries it: the house for two loses its wall the moment he lies. Cut this and the viewer loses the rule behind her want, that she will only be in the picture with a man who tells the truth, and the night plays as seduction instead of a trial she hopes he passes.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Dialogue: practical, live sync; no generated lip-sync anywhere.; Straw removal, practical. Acceptance: the base straw stays in its wet ring when the wall comes out.; Duffel glance. Acceptance: the zip gap reads; no label or logo.; The lie line is new, not Lucas's (writer's flag). Play it plain.

### SC07

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her fingertip as it rests on top of the chimney straw, then slides away, and the straw stays standing: the same rest-and-slide as her thumb on the flip icon. She wants a reason to let him pass; JEAN wants her home tonight, so he takes her turn. He brings the candle to her in two moves so he can see her eyes, and his eyes stay on hers through both. She holds his eyes and blinks less than he does. After 'tame a beast' and 'What dog doesn't want a true owner?' he half-stands off his stool and leans across in front of her for the caddy, and awards himself a straw without asking; the flirt is in the lean. Then 'Who would have known a stranger could be my next lover?', the 3-second vision, and one shake of her head brings her back to the bar.
- **blocking**: Continuous from SC06: his coupe at x 140, y 350, hers at x 140, y 440, PR-33 at x 140, y 320, and the house at S3 (base only, x 148, y 385-405). JEAN on stool 2 (y 350) faces the counter, west; PR-31 at x 115, y 260 is 90 cm to his left (south). His left hand draws it north along the counter to x 115, y 350, in front of him. His right hand pushes it on to x 112, y 418 on her side, 13 cm off the house's roof line. Both moves stay west of the glasses and the base straw. The caddy (x 130, y 480) is about 130 cm to his right: he half-stands off stool 2 and leans north across in front of her, his arm and shoulder passing over the counter edge between her and the counter, draws a straw and sits back. He pushes it 1 cm into PR-31's wax pool at the rim on his side of the wick (east), so it stands 19 cm above the wax, leaning 5° toward him (PR-30 S1). Her right fingertip goes on top of it, then off. VENUS stays on stool 3 (y 440), turned fully to him.
- **action progression**: His left hand, closed on PR-31's glass at the SC06 cut, draws it north to in front of him (1.0); the flame leans back against the move and settles as his right hand takes the glass (0.5); his right hand pushes it on to x 112, y 418 (1.0); the flame leans back again and settles (0.5). It leans twice, once per move. Dialogue from 'Let's go deeper' to 'What dog doesn't want a true owner?'. He half-stands and leans across in front of her (1.0), draws a straw from the caddy (0.5) and sits back (0.8). He leans right and stands it in the wax (1.0) and lets go; it stands (0.5). Her fingertip rests on its top (1.0) and slides off (0.5); it stays. Her line. Insert, 3.0 s at real speed: her hand on a black shirt against white sheets. Back at the bar, one shake of her head (0.8).
- **emotional shift**: Before: the judge, eyes steady, fingertip over the straws and ready to pull. Trigger: she watches him stand his own straw upright. After: her fingertip rests on it and leaves it standing, the vision comes, and she shakes it off. From judge to lover.
- **transition in**: Cut on action from SC06: his left hand closed on PR-31's glass, the draw starting in the same instant.
- **transition out**: Hard cut from her head shake to the silent street outside the window. The bar sound drops to a muffled street.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' Her fingertip resting on PR-30 and sliding off carries it: the flip-icon gesture, made while he is alive, on the one straw he stood up for them. Cut this and the viewer loses that her hesitation is want, not caution, and her thumb in the car would read as a killer's discipline.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Candle moves. Acceptance: two moves, one hand each, left then right; the glass never lifts off the counter; the flame leans twice and settles; nothing on the counter is touched on the way.; Straw about 3 cm from a live flame. Method: practical, at the pool's rim on Jean's side of the wick and leaning away from the flame, with a fire-safety check on set. Acceptance: no scorch, smoke or curl at the end of the take.; Straw in soft wax. Method: practical, with a hidden stiffener if production design needs one. Acceptance: it stands 19 cm, leans 5° and survives the fingertip rest.; The lean across her. Acceptance: his arm crosses in front of her over the counter edge without touching her or the straws; practical, blocked with both actors.; Vision insert: 3.0 s at 24 fps real speed; no slow motion, no nudity; her hand (right-hand scar), a #111111 shirt, white sheets. Practical, with an intimacy coordinator.; Candle flame: practical pillar only.

### SC08

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on his phone in her hands as she turns it round so its screen faces Jean and shoots him on his own front camera, her face hidden behind it. Through the window, play a real flirt and cut on the laughs: he adds straws and she pulls them. At last call Jean holds his phone out on its front camera to fit the two of them and the chimney in one frame. She takes it without a word, pleasantly and quickly, turns it round with both hands and holds it out at him, so the screen now shows only him. He preens at his own image, chin up, a half grin, while she stays behind the phone. She never touches his flip icon; that press is saved for SC18. Real detail: he checks the photo of himself, laughs and pockets it, too vain to notice she is missing from it. His last reach is for the chimney, to take home as a trophy, and she plucks it first.
- **blocking**: Window montage from the street side of wall A's window (x 40-440), the house at x 130, y 395 through the glass. His fresh coupe is set down on the base straw over the dried ring; it rocks; he lifts it off, laughing, and sets it back at x 140, y 350, re-wetting the ring (PR-32 S3). The house grows to S4 (six straws), then S5 as a roof straw is drawn toward the counter edge, with PR-30 upright in the wax beside it (the S03 frame), then S6. At last call the counter is bare except for PR-31, with PR-30 standing at x 112, y 418. JEAN on stool 2 holds PR-35 out at arm's length in his right hand, front camera on, screen toward the two of them. VENUS on stool 3 takes it in her right hand, brings her left hand to it and turns it round, screen to Jean. She leans back on stool 3 and holds it out at chest height about 60 cm from his face, PR-30 at the frame's edge between them, her face behind the phone from his side. She hands it back into his right hand. The BARTENDER reaches for PR-32 and PR-33. She plucks PR-30 into her jacket's inside left pocket beside PR-03. Jean shoulders PR-05. Exit: arms tangled, south through the glazed door to the curb. Her two-seater stays across the street, top up.
- **action progression**: Window: five or six silent shots of 1.5-3.0 s, every cut on a laugh; the coupe rock (1.5); the roof-straw pull (1.5). Last call: he holds his phone out (1.0); she takes it in her right hand (0.8); both hands turn it round, screen to him (0.6); she leans back and holds it out at him (0.6); he preens at the screen, chin up (1.5); her right index finger presses the side button (0.3); she hands it back (0.8); he looks, laughs and pockets it (2.0). The bartender reaches (1.0) as Jean goes for the chimney (0.8). She plucks it first (0.5) and pockets it inside her jacket (0.8). He shoulders the duffel (1.0); they stand and tangle arms (2.0) and walk out (5.0).
- **emotional shift**: Hers. Before: laughing, building and pulling straws. Trigger: his front camera frames the two of them, the picture she wants, offered on his terms by a man who lied. After: she turns his camera on him alone, stays behind it, and keeps the last straw. His: he wanted a picture of both of them, gets one of himself, and is pleased.
- **transition in**: Hard cut to the window exterior, silent.
- **transition out**: Cut on action: the bar door swings shut behind them, and SC09 picks them up on the curb as it closes.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-35 carries it: he offers her a photograph of the two of them, and she turns his own camera round on him and stays behind it. Cut this and the viewer loses that she could have the picture tonight and refuses it, because it has to be the right one, taken by her. That refusal makes SC18's press an ending, not a habit.
- **practical or compositing flags**: PR-35, the hero beat. Acceptance: her thumb never touches PR-35's flip icon; the screen faces Jean for the whole shot; the shutter is the side button, pressed by her right index finger. PD confirms PR-35's case exposes a side button. The screen image is composited if seen, with no legible UI text.; S03 is this scene's roof-straw pull; a generated still is high-risk because straws bend, multiply and cross. Method: a practical tabletop shoot, or CG straws over a bar plate. Acceptance: exactly 5 flat straws plus 1 under her fingertip, straight and uncrossed; the base in the re-wet ring; PR-30 upright in the wax (it may sit at the frame edge); two coupes; a normal fingertip; no text.; Phone handed between people, twice. Method: practical only, never generated. Acceptance: PR-35's split case and cracked screen tell it apart from PR-01.; Window glass. Acceptance: the house reads through the glass, white on the dark counter.; No slow motion on the laughs.

### SC09

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her left hand as it closes on his wrist at the tin and moves his hand to her waist while she laughs against his mouth. She wants the tin kept hers and her face out of the dash cam; JEAN wants his hands inside her jacket. At the curb she leans in and types the drop-off on his phone herself, a corner and not a door, while he holds it. Real detail: she never takes the phone from him. In the car she takes the seat behind the DRIVER. The DRIVER wants his back seat kept clean: he flicks the dome light on without turning his head. When she flicks it off he leaves it off, and his eyes go to the mirror for 2 s at a time. When her eyes open they stay on Jean and never go to the dash-cam lens.
- **blocking**: LOC-05 curb, then LOC-06. At the curb JEAN holds PR-35 out in his right hand, and VENUS leans in at his shoulder to type. Nothing changes hands. Cabin: she gets in first by the curb-side door and slides across to y 100, directly behind the DRIVER; JEAN sits at y 40 by the door. Her inside left pocket (PR-03, PR-30) is on her far side; PR-01 is in her right chest pocket. PR-05 lies on its side in the footwell (x 72-100, y 45-95). PR-36 sits at x 172, y 110, lens rearward: Jean in the curb-side corner faces it, and she, leaning into him, gives it the back of her head. PR-37 is at x 55, y 75, within her reach; the master rocker is on the driver's console.
- **action progression**: Curb: he holds his phone out (1.0); she leans in and types (3.0); he pockets it (0.8); the car pulls up. She gets in and slides across (2.0); he follows (1.5); the door shuts. They kiss with their clothes on, the overlay running from 10/23/2026 01:47:12. The driver's right hand goes to the console rocker and the dome comes on (0.8). Her right hand goes up to the lens rocker and switches it off (1.0) without breaking the kiss, and the frame drops to grain and passing streetlight. The driver looks to the mirror (2.0), then back to the road. Jean's right hand goes inside her jacket, across to the inside left pocket (1.0); his fingers find the tin's edge through the lining (0.5). Her left hand closes on his wrist (0.4) and moves it to her waist (0.8); she laughs against his mouth (1.0). Her eyes stay open to the stop. The car stops at a corner, and they get out, the duffel on his shoulder (4.0).
- **emotional shift**: Before: eyes shut, all in. Trigger: his hand on the tin's hard edge, visible through the jacket. After: her eyes open, she steers his hand away and laughs, and her eyes stay open until the car stops.
- **transition in**: The bar door closing bridges the cut to the curb.
- **transition out**: Hard cut from the dark street corner to the white marble lobby (SC10). The jump in brightness carries the move into her world; the first sound is the brass street door closing.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-36 carries it: the only recording of the two of them tonight belongs to a stranger, and she keeps her face out of it. Cut this and the viewer loses that not every picture of them counts; hers has to be taken by her, on her terms. They also lose the moment the tin nearly slips out of her control.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Dash-cam overlay: composite '10/23/2026 01:47:12' verbatim, running; no speed, GPS or brand.; His phone at the curb: practical; the screen is never legible, and she types while he holds it.; Clothed kissing: practical, with an intimacy coordinator; nothing from it goes into the pitch pieces.; Dome light, two rockers, practical. Acceptance: on for under 3 s.; Car: no badges or rideshare trade dress (A10).

### SC10

- **performance beats**: Jean's scene. Hero beat: close on the shoes as he stops in black on the white threshold and toes them off, and her hand lines them up on the white mat beside his duffel. He wants to know who she is and how she pays for this, but he wants the night more. His eyes go to the surfaces first: marble, brass. 'Mind the step' cuts off 'So what do you-'; he looks down at the curtail step, and the question is gone. VENUS leads a step ahead, never looks back, and plays 'It's a little messy' as a real apology in a spotless apartment. Inside, while he deals with his shoes, she hangs her jacket and takes her phone, the tin and the straw into her left hand, unhurried and in plain view, the way you empty your pockets at home. She answers him from a crouch, already setting his shoes square. Once they are squared she stands, steps back to the distance she holds her phone at, and looks at them, the phone in her hand and not raised.
- **blocking**: LOC-07: in through the street double door (wall A, x 300-480), along the gloss path to the curtail step (x 660). Flight 1 runs north along wall D to the half landing, and flight 2 south to the floor-2 landing. PR-39 is on the landing's east side, opening inward. She stays a step ahead. LOC-08: she opens the door and steps in ahead of him to PR-62 (wall A, x 160, about 1 m from the door); JEAN stops on the threshold. PR-40 is at x 65-145, y 5-55; PR-05 goes flat at x 150-200, y 5-33, under PR-62. The bathroom door is on wall B at y 120-200, 1.9 m from the threshold, on his left. She crouches at the mat's south end, her left hand holding PR-01, PR-03 and PR-30 against her chest, and her right hand sets the shoes. After squaring PR-41 she steps back about 60 cm into the room and looks.
- **action progression**: Lobby crossing (6.0), his eyes on the floor and the mailboxes. The line is cut off at the step (1.0). Stairs (20.0), with dialogue on flight 2. From her hand on the lever, 0.0: she opens PR-39 (1.5). 1.5-2.5: she steps in to PR-62 while he stops on the threshold. 1.5-3.5: he shrugs PR-05 to the floor (2.0). 2.5-3.7: she shrugs off her jacket (1.2); 3.7-4.3: hangs it by its collar loop (0.6). 3.5-5.5: he toes off his shoes, heel against toe, and they land askew on PR-40 (2.0). 4.3-4.8: her right hand takes PR-01 from the jacket's right chest pocket into her left hand (0.5); 4.8-5.6: takes PR-03 and PR-30 from the inside left pocket into the same hand (0.8). 5.5-8.0: JEAN: 'I happened to miss the mess. Can I use your bathroom?' 8.0-11.5: VENUS crouches and answers, 'Of course. Right on the left. I'm gonna change and make us something to drink.' while her right hand sets the shoes side by side, toes to the door, 2 cm apart (8.0-10.5) and draws PR-05 flat beside the mat by one handle (10.5-11.5). Meanwhile Jean walks 1.9 m to the bathroom door (8.0-10.0), opens it (0.8), steps in (0.8) and pulls it shut (0.7). 11.5-12.3: she stands, steps back and looks, and the latch clicks at 12.3, inside her look.
- **emotional shift**: Jean's. Before: eyes on the surfaces, starting to ask. Trigger: 'Mind the step', then the white room. After: no more questions, only a joke and a request for the bathroom. Venus holds; her plan is running.
- **transition in**: Hard cut from the dark street to the white marble.
- **transition out**: Hard cut on the latch click, heard over her look at 12.3: SC11 opens inside on the shut door, with the click's tail crossing the cut. The eye goes from his shoes on the mat to the back of the bathroom door.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' Her step back carries it: after squaring PR-41 she steps back to her phone distance and looks at his black shoes in her white doorway (0.8), with the phone in her hand and not raised. Cut it and the viewer loses her framing him into her home as if through a lens without lifting one, the composing that SC17's posed armchair and SC18's photograph finish.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Jacket items. Acceptance: PR-01, PR-03 and PR-30 come out of the hanging jacket on screen and stay in her left hand to the end of the scene; nothing is pocketed; the tin stays shut.; Latch sync. Acceptance: the click lands inside her 0.8 s look, not before it and not on a cut.; Lobby and landing: no legible directory, names or numbers.; Shoes and duffel: no logos, tags or branded zip pulls (stylist and production designer confirm).

### SC11
