- **performance beats**: Jean's scene. Hero beat: close on the tissue as he looks at the faint pink on it, drops it in the bowl and flushes. He wants to get back out to her fast. He checks his teeth before he deals with the blood. He lifts the seat, stops half a second at the drops, then uses the toilet anyway, back to the lens and heard only: the blood doesn't stop him, and his wipe afterwards reads as routine courtesy. When the track starts through the door he turns his head to it and grins, because he thinks it is for him. Real detail: at the end he flattens his own collar points in the mirror with two fingers, the gesture she will make on him dead. No question crosses his face.
- **blocking**: LOC-09. JEAN comes in by the door (wall A) to PR-49 above the basin (wall D), then crosses 1.4 m to PR-47 (wall C, bowl front at y 190), where he stands with his back to the door and the camera kept behind him. The roll is on wall B at y 200, beside him. He goes to the basin tap and back, then to the basin and mirror, and out. The seat is left up, and the door is left open behind him. Heard off screen only: the track from PR-54 in the bedroom and the ice at the kitchen run (SC12).
- **action progression**: Slot 60.0. 0.0: the latch clicks (SC10's last frame). 0.0-3.0: at the mirror, grin and teeth check (3.0). 3.0-4.5: to the toilet (1.5). 4.5-5.0: lifts the seat (0.5). 5.0-5.5: sees the drops and stops (0.5). 5.5-6.5: turns his back to the lens and unzips (1.0). 6.5-28.5: uses the toilet, sound only, head tipped back, one hand flat on the tile (22.0); one cut inside, at 17.5, to the shut door from inside, silence beyond it. 28.5-30.0: zips and turns (1.5). 30.0-31.0: tears off three sheets (1.0). 31.0-32.5: to the basin (1.5). 32.5-34.0: tap on and wets the sheets (1.5); at 33.3 the track starts low through the door (SC12 0.3). 34.0-34.5: tap off (0.5). 34.5-36.0: turns his head to the door and grins (1.5). 36.0-37.0: back to the toilet (1.0). 37.0-39.5: wipes the rim, working at the dried crust (2.5). 39.5-41.5: looks at the pink (2.0); the ice drops through the door at 40.5-42.0 (SC12 7.5-9.0). 41.5-42.0: drops the tissue in the bowl (0.5). 42.0-42.5: flushes, after the ice (0.5). 42.5-43.5: to the basin (1.0). 43.5-48.5: washes his hands (5.0). 48.5-49.5: dries them on his pants (1.0). 49.5-52.5: flattens his collar points in the mirror and grins (3.0). 52.5-54.0: to the door (1.5). 54.0-55.5: hand on the handle, one last look at the mirror (1.5). 55.5: opens it (SC12 22.5) and walks out (1.0). 56.5-60.0: hold on the empty bathroom, seat up, the pink haze on the rim, the track through the open door (3.5).
- **emotional shift**: Before: grinning at himself. Trigger: the drops under the seat. After: a half-second stop, then he uses the toilet, wipes the drops away, washes his hands and walks out smiling. He removes whatever doesn't fit the night he wants.
- **transition in**: Hard cut on the latch click from SC10; the door has just shut, seen from inside.
- **transition out**: Hard cut back in time: SC12 opens at SC11's 33.0 in the bedroom. The sound rewinds with it: the track heard through the open door at SC11's end cuts out, and the running tap returns through the wall. His exit is seen again from her side at SC12 22.5.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' SC11 carries plot, not the idea: the drops on PR-47 are the previous man's (A13), the first sign that Jean is not the first man here. Cut it and the viewer loses that history and the pink tissue, but not the idea, which SC18 carries. Staged as written; blocker filed with the writer.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Dried drops. Method: practical makeup/SFX, 2-4 mm of #6E1C18 crust. Acceptance: after the wipe, a haze of 10 x 3 mm at most; no smear, run or spray.; The wet wipe (PR-48 S2) makes the scripted pink physically true; keep the tap beat.; Toilet use: back to the lens, sound only, clothed from behind; no exposure in any frame.; Mirror. Acceptance: only Jean is reflected; no crew or camera.; Sync with SC12. Acceptance: the track start at 33.3, the ice at 40.5 and the door at 55.5 match SC12's 0.3, 7.5 and 22.5 to the frame; the tap is running at SC12 0.0-1.0.

### SC12

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her fingertip landing on the tin's lid when he tries it. She wants to quiet herself before he comes out, then to ask the last question first; JEAN wants the bedroom now. The track is her tool: she starts it, and once the loop comes round her hands go still on the two squared glasses. It is seen, never explained. With him she plays it light and dry: 'You haven't shown me anything worth missing.' He downs his drink like a shot and blows out, breath only. The chimney straw stands in her ice: the last straw, standing again. When he steers her toward the bedroom she stops at the chair and sits, and her fingertip holds the lid. 'You feel like this space isn't safe?' is asked warmly.

From the nightstand she walks out through the bedroom door, down the hall to the kitchen run (x 640-700), about 10 m. She ices and pours, then carries both tumblers north across the room and stands beside PR-44 at the table's east end. JEAN walks from the bathroom door (wall B, y 120-200) along the sofa to its east end (y 540-590). Seated: VENUS in PR-44 facing west, knees about 35 cm from his; JEAN at the sofa's east end facing south. PR-03 ends at x 140, y 525, hinge south, seam toward him.

SC12 0.0-1.0: in the bedroom, the tap heard through the wall, she presses PR-54's dot, and the track starts at 0.3; she turns the ring to about 20% (1.0). 1.0-7.5: to the kitchen run by the hall (6.5). 7.5-9.0: ice into both tumblers with the tongs (1.5). 9.0-11.0: pours both (2.0). 11.0-12.0: stands PR-30 from the counter in her ice (1.0). 12.0-16.0: carries both to PR-42 (4.0). 16.0-17.0: squares them side by side at x 160, y 500 and y 520 (1.0). 17.0-19.5: her fingertips keep nudging the two bases into line (2.5). 19.5-22.5: the loop comes round on its downbeat (0.3 + 2 x 9.6) and her hands go still on the glasses (3.0). 22.5: the bathroom door opens; she lifts both glasses (0.8) and turns to him (0.7). He crosses (3.0). Lines. He takes his glass from her right hand (0.8), downs it (1.5), blows out (1.0) and sets it at x 140, y 490 (0.5). Lines. His hand goes to her waist (0.5) and steers her a step toward the hall (1.0). She stops at PR-44, sets her glass at x 175, y 520 with the straw standing (0.8) and sits (1.5); he sits at the sofa's east end (1.5). Her right fingertip slides PR-03 to x 140, y 525 (1.0). He turns it over (1.2), sets it back hinge south, seam toward him (0.5), and puts his thumbnail to the seam (0.3). Her fingertip lands on the lid (0.3); he presses and it holds (0.8); he lets go, grins and sits back (1.0). Lines to 'No. Not at all.' Her fingertip stays on the lid. Dialogue fills the slot to 120.0.
- **emotional shift**: Hers first. Before: quick hands with the ice, pour, straw and carry, still nudging the glasses into line. Trigger, heard: the loop comes round on its downbeat at 19.5. After: her hands rest still on the glasses for 3.0 s, the only rest before he comes out. Then his. Before: grabbing, at the drink and then the tin. Trigger: her fingertip holding the lid down. After: hands off, he sits back and waits for her.
- **transition in**: Hard cut back 27.0 s from SC11's end to SC11's 33.0; the running tap, heard through the wall where the track just was, marks the rewind.
- **transition out**: Hard cut in continuous time to SC13's wider shot as she begins 'Trust is a thing that is earned', her fingertip on the lid matching across the cut.

### SC13

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her hands. Her left hand draws the straw from her ice and lays it flat, leaving a wet mark; her right fingertip lifts off the tin and her right thumb opens it toward him. What's inside stays out of frame. She wants one true thing about love, his last straw; JEAN wants the bedroom. She asks her speech as a real question, warmly. His left hand goes to her right thigh. While he reaches for a comeback, his eyes go to the tin twice. On 'That's for you to find out' he grins, thinking he has won. Her verdict lands with no change of face; only her hands move.

Jean leans in from the sofa's east end; his left hand goes to her right thigh. Her left hand draws PR-30 from her tumbler (x 175, y 520) and lays it flat at x 160, y 488-508, leaving a 20 x 1.2 cm wet mark. Her right thumb raises the lid from the north seam about 100° on its south hinge, so the open well faces Jean. The camera stays south of the raised lid and no higher than its top edge. The track plays low from the bedroom throughout.
- **action progression**: Her speech (dialogue). He leans in (1.0) and puts his hand on her thigh (0.5). His speech, eyes going to the tin twice. Her question; his line. The verdict: her left hand draws PR-30 out (1.0) and lays it flat (1.0); her right fingertip lifts (0.5); her right thumb raises the lid (1.0). Hold on his face (2.0). Cut.
- **emotional shift**: Before: fingertip on the lid, straw standing, face open and warm, hoping. Trigger: 'That's for you to find out', a line instead of a truth. After: her hands lay the straw flat and open the tin while her face stays pleasant.
- **transition in**: Hard cut in continuous time from SC12.
- **transition out**: Hard cut with an elision from the open lid straight to SC14, with Jean already lifting her. What he takes from the tin, and how, stays off screen.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-30 laid flat and PR-03 opening carry it: the hope of a picture with him alive goes down with the last straw, because he gave her a line. Cut this and the viewer loses why he dies and that she wanted him to pass, and SC18's photo would read as a trophy.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Tin interior. Acceptance: no frame ever shows into the well; the camera stays south of the raised lid and below its top edge. No contents, preparation or dosing (must avoid).; Wet mark on white gloss, practical. Acceptance: a streak with no pooled liquid.; Hand on thigh: practical, with an intimacy coordinator.

### SC14

- **performance beats**: Jean's scene. Hero beat: close on his black-socked foot as it stops at the three drops, then steps over them. JEAN wants to get her to the bed; VENUS wants him to keep walking, and she never looks down. She turns his face back to hers with her right hand and says 'J.', the gesture she will repeat on him dead (V01-B07, SC16). Here, alive, he looks back.
- **blocking**: JEAN stands at the sofa's east end and lifts VENUS from PR-44. He carries her past the table's east end, with the open tin and flat straw in view as they pass, south across the rug to the hall, then east to the bedroom door (wall C, x 540-630). PR-45 is at x 575-600, y 880-886, on the hall side of the threshold. He stops with his lead foot short of the drops, then steps over into LOC-10. The track grows as they near the bedroom (source only).
- **action progression**: He stands (1.0) and lifts her (2.0). The carry, about 7 m (8.0). He looks down and stops for half a step (0.8). Her hand turns his face to hers (0.8); 'J.' (0.5). He steps over (1.0) and through the door (1.5).
- **emotional shift**: Jean's. Before: striding, eyes on her. Trigger: the drops at his feet. After: a half-step stop, eyes down. Second trigger: her hand on his jaw and 'J.' After that: eyes up; he steps over and never looks back. Venus holds: she never looks down, because she doesn't know the drops are there.
- **transition in**: Hard cut with an elision from the open tin.
- **transition out**: Match cut: his black foot stepping over the drops on the white floor becomes his black shirt lying on the white bedroom floor just inside the door (x 200, y 60), later that night (SC15). The match is black on white at floor level.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' Her hand turning his face toward hers carries it: the last time he looks back. Cut this and the viewer loses the gesture's living use, so her turning his dead head toward her in V01-B07 and on the pillow in SC16 has nothing to answer.
- **practical or compositing flags**: The carry. Method: practical, rehearsed with a stunt coordinator. Acceptance: steady, no stumble.; Drops: 3-5 mm of dried crust that reads only in the close shot; no smear.

### SC15

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her right hand as it passes her phone on the nightstand, without slowing, and turns up the speaker ring over his catching breath. She wants to stop hearing his breathing. Tactic 1: she folds his black clothes square and sets his sunglasses on top. Tactic 2: she washes her hands at the basin with her eyes down. Real detail: she never meets the mirror, and the cabinet stays shut. Tactic 3: she lies down. His breathing stops for a long gap, then catches with a rasp, and her hand goes past the phone to the ring. Then, for the first time, her hands go still. JEAN: breathing only, face down, recorded wild.
- **blocking**: LOC-10. JEAN lies face down on the south side of PR-52; VENUS is on the north side. PR-56 is on the floor: the shirt at x 200, y 60 with PR-04 hooked in its placket, the pants at x 280, y 150. She folds them onto PR-55 (x 330-380, y 60-110), with PR-04 folded, lenses up, on the shirt. She goes out through the hall, past the threshold drops without looking, to the LOC-09 basin (wall D), and back. In bed she lies on her back, head to wall C, her right side toward PR-53 (x 60-110, y 365-405). PR-01 lies face down nearer the bed (x 98, y 385) and PR-54 farther from it (x 75, y 385).
- **action progression**: Slot 150.0. The track has played low from PR-54 since SC12. The editor phases the 9.6 s loop so its downbeats fall at 150.0 minus multiples of 9.6 (92.4, 102.0, 111.6, 121.2, 130.8, 140.4, 150.0), with each bar-4 hole 7.2-9.6 s into its loop. 0.0-4.0: match-cut hold on the shirt, his slow breathing off screen (4.0). 4.0-14.0: she lies awake on her back through two of his breaths (10.0). 14.0-16.5: sits up, feet to the floor (2.5). 16.5-21.3: to the shirt (3.0); gathers it (1.0); unhooks PR-04 from the placket (0.8). 21.3-24.3: to the pants (2.0); gathers them (1.0). 24.3-37.5: to PR-55 (2.0); folds PR-04 and sets it on the seat corner (0.5); folds both garments into one square (8.0); sets it square, shirt on top, and squares the edges with both palms (1.5); PR-04 onto the shirt, lenses up (1.2). 37.5-45.5: walk to the bathroom (8.0). 45.5-60.0: light on (0.5); tap on (0.6); she washes her hands, eyes on the basin (6.0); tap off (0.6); wet hands flat on the basin edge, eyes down, his breathing carrying through the open door (5.8); light off and turn (1.0). 60.0-68.0: walk back (8.0). 68.0-70.0: lies down (2.0). 70.0-88.6: lies still, eyes on the back of his head, four of his breaths (18.6). 88.6-99.6: his breathing stops (11.0). 99.6-100.4: the rasp, in the bar-4 hole (0.8), then ragged breathing. 100.4-101.4: her right hand leaves the sheet and passes over PR-01 without slowing to the ring (1.0). 101.4-103.4: turns the ring to about 80% (2.0). 103.4-104.2: hand back to her side (0.8). 104.2-142.6: four loops loud (38.4). She lies still, eyes on the back of his head, and his breathing is no longer heard. One cut inside, on the 121.2 downbeat, from the bed wide to her still right hand on the sheet. 142.6-143.1: her eyes close for the first time (0.5). 143.1-150.0: hold (6.9); cut on the 150.0 downbeat.
- **emotional shift**: Before: hands busy, folding and washing. Trigger, heard: his breath stops for 11 s and catches with a rasp. After: her hand passes the phone, the one thing that could call for help, and turns the music up until his breathing is under it. Then her hands go still, and at the end her eyes close.
- **transition in**: Match cut from SC14, black on white at floor level.
- **transition out**: Hard cut to morning on the 150.0 downbeat with the track continuous: the same four bars run across the cut, and the light on the same bed goes from night to morning.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' Her hand passing PR-01 for PR-54 carries it. The phone is her camera and the one thing that could call for help. Cut this and the viewer loses that she let him die while he could still be helped, so her waiting in SC16 and the photo in SC18 become love for a man she let go, not a collector's calm.
- **practical or compositing flags**: No meds in SC15: the cabinet stays shut, and she never meets the mirror. Acceptance: no crew and no Venus reflection in any frame.; Breathing: wild sound, no lip-sync, face down. The 11.0 s stop sits inside a take, not across a cut.; Rasp in the bar-4 hole (99.6-102.0), so it reads over the low track. Acceptance: audible with the track at 20%.; Music: source only (A08). Levels change only when her hand turns the ring.; Four-loop hold (104.2-142.6). Acceptance, tested after degrain: nothing moves but her breathing and one blink; live grain over the whole frame; the inside cut lands on a downbeat.; Speaker and phone back stay blank; no screen is lit.

### SC16

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on his arm as she lifts it over her waist and it slides off. She wants him to turn over and reach for her. The look follows ref 06 as the brief describes it: he lies face down, mouth open, and she lies awake with a hand to her forehead, eyes on the back of his head. This is the one stretch where her hands have nothing to do. Tactic 1: she waits through three loops. Tactic 2: the arm. Tactic 3: 'J. Stay for breakfast.', at the dry pitch of 'That's a drink', not pleading. Tactic 4: she turns him over herself and sets his face toward her on the pillow, with the care she gave his collar. Then she switches the music off, gets up and pulls the first corner off the bed. JEAN: absolute stillness, no breath; from the roll on, his head stays where she sets it.
- **blocking**: LOC-10. PR-52 S1: JEAN face down on the south side, mouth open, right arm off the bed on that side, left arm toward her. VENUS on the north side, on her back, head to wall C. PR-54 loops at about 80%, with PR-01 face down beside it on PR-53, north of the bed. She kneels on her side of the bed to roll him toward her. After the roll (PR-52 S2) he lies on his back, face turned north toward her on the pillow. She ends standing at her side's head corner (x 124, y 420), the fitted corner in her right hand.
- **action progression**: Slot 90.0. The 9.6 s loop's downbeats fall at 0, 9.6, 19.2, 28.8, 38.4, 48.0, 57.6 and 67.2. 0.0-28.8: three loops. She lies awake, hand at her forehead, eyes on the back of his head; nothing moves but her breathing. One cut inside, on 19.2, from the bed wide to her hand. 28.8-35.1: she rolls onto her left side toward him (1.0); her right hand takes his left wrist (0.5); lifts his arm over her waist (1.5); holds her hand over his (2.0); lets go (0.3); the arm slides off onto the sheet (1.0). 35.1-45.8: she lies facing him (10.7). 45.8-47.8: 'J. Stay for breakfast.', inside the 45.6-48.0 bar-4 hole (2.0). 47.8-57.6: she waits; no answer (9.8). 57.6-62.6: up onto her knees (1.2); her right hand to his far shoulder and her left to his hip (1.0); she pulls, and he rolls onto his back toward her, dead weight, his right arm coming over onto the sheet between them (2.0); she sits back on her heels (0.8). 62.6-65.0: her right hand turns his face toward her on the pillow (1.0), stays (1.0) and withdraws (0.4). 65.0-67.2: she reaches past PR-01 to PR-54 and holds her finger on the dot (1.8); presses on the 67.2 downbeat, so the next bar never lands (0.4). 67.2-79.0: silence. On her heels, hands flat on her thighs, she looks at his face (11.8). 79.0-81.0: off the bed, feet to the floor (2.0). 81.0-82.5: to the head corner (1.5). 82.5-83.0: right hand grips the fitted corner (0.5). 83.0-84.5: pulls it off the mattress corner (1.5). 84.5-90.0: holds the loose corner and looks along the bed at him (5.5). Hard cut.
- **emotional shift**: Before: still and waiting, a hand at her forehead, eyes on him. Triggers: his arm slides off her waist (seen), and her line gets no answer (heard). After: she stops waiting. She turns him over herself, sets his face toward her, ends the music on a downbeat and takes hold of the bed to strip it.
- **transition in**: The music bridges the cut from SC15 on the downbeat.
- **transition out**: Hard cut on the loose corner to SC17's silent LOC-11 insert; the silence carries across.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' His arm lifted over her waist and his face set toward her on the pillow carry it: she stages the picture of a couple waking up together, and he can't hold it. Cut this and the viewer loses the want at its plainest, feelings returned rather than sex (limerence), and the picture she can't make even here.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Jean's stillness. Method: practical breath-hold takes, or VFX paint-out of breathing. Acceptance, tested after degrain: no rise in the chest and no movement anywhere on him across the take, then live grain restored over the whole frame.; Arm slide. Method: practical, dead weight. Acceptance: it slides off rather than dropping stiffly.; The roll. Method: practical, a stillness-trained actor or body double, rehearsed with a stunt coordinator and an intimacy coordinator, with a modesty sheet. Acceptance: no effort beat from him, no visible help, and his head never moves relative to his shoulders except under her hand.; Dead eyes from the roll on: the V01 makeup lock, no blink, lids at the top of the iris.; Switch-off on the 67.2 downbeat. Acceptance: no partial bar is heard after the press.; Ref 06 is a look only, with no likeness. The bedding is white, not patterned, because the color theme wins.

### SC17

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on the cloth as she scrubs out the three drops she missed until it is pink and the floor is white, then folds it square before it goes into the trash. She wants the apartment spotless and nothing of him kept before she takes him away. She dresses him unhurried; his limbs go where her hands put them, and she finishes by flattening his collar points with two fingers. In the bathroom she cleans the cabinet with the same face and hands she gave the padlock. She lifts the organizer, flips the lid, wipes its rim round the fill slip, wipes the shelf and sets it back square. There is no wince and no pause on the full compartments or the date, so the meds are seen and never the reason. The threshold is the only place she speeds up. Real detail: the pink cloth folded into a 10 x 10 cm square before it goes into the trash, with the last straw dropped on top of it. From here the head-alone rule holds.
- **blocking**: Insert: LOC-11. PR-63 is down and PR-64's doors are open. LOC-01 is top down and clean, passenger seat empty, nose to the roller door; nobody in frame. LOC-10: she dresses him where he lies on his back on PR-52, from the pile on PR-55. After the cut he sits in PR-44 (x 110-190, y 555-635, facing west), hands on the arms. PR-05 is by the door (x 150-200, y 5-33). LOC-09: she stands at the basin under PR-49; PR-47 stays out of frame. Threshold: walking east from the hall, she kneels on the hall side of PR-45 (x 575-600, y 880-886), facing east. PR-60 is at x 650-680, y 470-500. PR-42 holds only PR-03 (open, hinge south) and PR-30 (flat). She closes the tin from its south side, behind the raised lid. PR-62 is on wall A at x 160. She is out of frame for the final hold. VENUS_L02 has no pocket or waistband: PR-46 stays in her right hand from the kitchen run to PR-60, and PR-01 stays on PR-53 until her change.
- **action progression**: Slot 150.0. 0.0-4.0: LOC-11 insert, silent, nothing moving (4.0). 4.0-34.0, dressing, 30.0 s cut into: she lifts PR-04 off the pile and sets it folded on the seat corner (1.0); carries the clothes to the bed (2.0); sleeves up his arms (6.0); buttons bottom to top (7.0); pants elided on a cut; belt buckled (3.0); socks (3.0); shoes, fetched from PR-40 off screen, on and laced (5.0); two fingers flatten his collar points (2.0); PR-04 off the seat corner into her right hand (1.0). Hard cut; the move is elided. 34.0-37.0: at PR-44 she opens PR-04 with both hands and sets it on him (1.5); squares his hands on the arms (1.5). 37.0-43.5: to the duffel (4.0); main zip pulled shut (1.5); stood on end at x 160-188 (1.0). Hard cut to the bedroom. 43.5-73.5: strips the rest of PR-52 (5.0); bundles the sheets out of frame (2.0); fitted sheet on, four corners pulled tight (8.0); flat sheet and duvet (7.0); smooths with flat palms until no crease is left (7.0); steps back and looks (1.0). The tumblers and PR-59 are washed and put away off screen. 73.5-75.5: at the kitchen run, PR-46 into her right hand (2.0). Hard cut; the walk is elided. 75.5-82.3, LOC-09: opens PR-49 (0.8); her left hand lifts PR-50 (1.0); her left thumb flips its lid open (0.5); the cloth's corner wipes the lid's rim round the slip, with the date legible (1.0); she wipes the shelf (1.5); lid shut (0.4); sets it back square beside PR-51 (0.8); closes the mirror door with her eyes down (0.8). 82.3-84.3: the last 2 m of the hall toward the bedroom (2.0). 84.3-100.8: she looks down and stops (1.0); kneels (1.0); scrubs, faster (6.0); holds on the pink cloth and the white floor (1.0); folds the cloth square (1.5); stands (1.0); to PR-60 (4.0); pedal, drop, the lid soft-closes (1.0). 100.8-112.0: to PR-42 (4.0); picks up PR-30 (0.7); buffs the dried water mark off with the heel of her hand (1.5); back to PR-60 (4.0); pedal, and the straw drops onto the folded cloth (1.0). 112.0-117.0: cover cut to a close on Jean's black sleeves and hands squared on PR-44's white arms (5.0). It is an elision: her change into VENUS_L03 and PR-01 off PR-53 happen off screen, and Jean's stillness hides the minutes. 117.0-128.0: she re-enters in the pressed shirt (crease S1), PR-01 in her right hand. She pockets it in her right front jeans pocket (1.0); pushes the raised lid shut from behind it (0.8); picks up PR-03 (0.5); walks to PR-62 (4.0); puts the tin in the jacket's inside left pocket (1.5); takes the jacket off the hook over her left arm (1.0); steps out of frame past the entry (2.2). 128.0-150.0: hold on the finished room (22.0).
- **emotional shift**: Before: calm and methodical, never looking down. The cabinet is a hold: the padlock face, no change. Trigger, seen: the three drops at the threshold she missed. After: she kneels at once and scrubs harder and faster than at anything else, then folds the cloth square, back in control.
- **transition in**: Hard cut on SC16's loose corner to the silent LOC-11 insert, then a hard cut upstairs to her hands at his shirt sleeves.
- **transition out**: Match cut: Jean seated in black in the white armchair, sunglasses on (the hold), to Jean seated in black in PR-19 under his wall, sunglasses off (SC18). The white room's silence becomes the wind at the motel door.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-30 dropped into PR-60 on the pink cloth carries it: the last straw goes into the trash, and she keeps nothing of him. Jean posed in PR-44 is a portrait she makes and stays out of. Cut this and the viewer loses the proof that she is not a collector, so the photograph in SC18 would not be the only thing she keeps.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Meds (PR-50, PR-51). Acceptance: seen, never named. The only legible text is '09/18/2026' on the slip, during the 1.0 s rim wipe; all 28 compartments are full; the vial's label faces the wall; she never pauses on them. Risk: a pause or a close on the vial reads as the explanation (must avoid).; PR-47 stays out of every cabinet view until the writer adds the rim beat (world bible S5 pending).; Closing the tin. Acceptance: the camera stays south of PR-03, her hand closes the lid from behind it, and no frame shows into the well.; Dressing and seating the dead-weight actor: practical, with a body double, cut into and elided, played unhurried. Acceptance: no effort beat, and his head moves only under her hands.; Pink cloth. Acceptance: pink (#E3A9A6), not red, no drips; the scrubbed patch reads flatter and whiter than the satin floor.; Wardrobe continuity. Acceptance: in VENUS_L02, PR-46 is in her right hand in every frame from 75.5 to 99.8, and nothing is tucked or pocketed; she is in VENUS_L03 from 117.0.; Cover cut, 112.0-117.0. Acceptance: framed tight on his hands and sleeves, so it doesn't pre-empt the final wide.; LOC-11 insert. Acceptance: the car matches LOC-01; no text, numerals or signage.; Final hold, 22.0 s. Acceptance, tested after degrain: no motion in the room, the light or Jean, including his chest, then live grain and gate weave over the whole frame.; Taste note broken on purpose: no dish rack and no charging cable, because the tidiness is the horror.

### SC18

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her right thumb as it rests on the flip-camera icon and this time presses, and the screen shows both of them. She wants to get into one photograph with him. Her thumb rests as it did twice in the car. Trigger: she lifts her head from his shoulder, looks round past the back of his head at the next man's two prints she has just pinned, and holds: the wall has already moved on. She turns back, lays her head on his shoulder again and presses. For the first time her eyes go into a lens, her own front camera, and she is smiling. Two of her left fingers lift toward his chin and stop short. She doesn't touch him, doesn't lower the phone and plays no disappointment; the smile holds and she takes the photo, then taps print. Once she sits, she takes her hair down, and her curls fall loose onto his black shoulder before her head goes there. JEAN: the head-alone rule; his face stays where she set it at dusk, eyes open, no blink.
- **blocking**: LOC-04, continuous from V01-B14, with the wind at the door. JEAN sits in PR-19 (x 210, y 420) facing south, torso square, his head set 30° to his left (bearing about 150°, toward wall D). Entry: VENUS in VENUS_L03, hair tied with two elastics; PR-04 at her neckline; PR-02 grip-up in her right back pocket; PR-01 in her right front pocket; PR-61 in her left. PR-04 goes folded, lenses up, to x 200-214, y 365-370. At PR-06 by the jamb (x 195-240, y 22-50, below the held frame), PR-28 comes out and PR-02 goes in, muzzle toward wall B. PR-28 is pinned at x 305-315, heights 170-185 and 188-203, and the spare pin is parked in the pine at x 300, height 178. PR-18 moves to x 165, its seat edge touching PR-19's. She sits on his right (west), the side his face is turned from, head on his right shoulder. PR-01 is held at arm's length at about x 180, y 360, 25 cm above his eye line and tilted down, front lens to them, wall C behind. His gaze passes about 57° wide of the lens and below it, so both eyes read in three-quarter, aimed off to his left. Trigger look: over her left shoulder, past the back of his head, to PR-28. PR-01 then goes face up beside PR-25 (x 145, y 340), which ejects PR-27 south. PR-27 is pinned with the spare pin at x 290-295.4, heights 165-173.6, between Jean's photos (ending x 277.5) and PR-28.
- **action progression**: Slot 120.0. 0.0-4.0: match-cut hold on Jean in the chair (4.0). 4.0-6.8: unhooks PR-04 (1.0), folds it (1.0), sets it down (0.8). 6.8-9.8: to PR-06 (3.0). 9.8-15.6: bends and unzips it (1.0); her left hand lifts PR-28 out, pins through their tops (1.0); straightens (0.6); her right hand draws PR-02, muzzle down (0.8); she bends and lays it in, muzzle toward wall B (0.8); straightens (0.6); her right hand takes PR-01 out of her right front pocket while she is still standing (1.0). 15.6-22.1: to wall C (3.5); her left hand pins both prints and parks the spare pin, the phone in her right hand, screen to her palm (3.0). 22.1-26.6: behind the chairs to hers (1.5); her left hand moves PR-18 (1.5); sets PR-01 face down on the table's north edge at x 165 (0.5); sits (1.0). 26.6-30.4: seated, both hands take out the two elastics (1.4) and put them in the empty right front pocket (1.0); she leans toward him and her curls fall across his black shoulder (0.6); her head settles on it (0.8). 30.4-34.4: picks up PR-01 (1.0); raises it to arm's length (1.0); thumb to the icon (0.8); rests (1.2). 34.4-37.4: lifts her head and turns to PR-28 (1.0), holds (1.0), comes back to his shoulder (1.0). 37.4-43.2: presses (0.3); hold on the screen showing both (3.0); her left fingertips lift toward his chin (0.8), stop 2 cm short and hold (1.0), and lower (0.4); shutter (0.3). 43.2-48.7: she looks at the photo, head on his shoulder (5.5). 48.7-49.2: her thumb taps print (0.5). 49.2-50.2: sets PR-01 face up by the printer (1.0). 50.2-85.2: the printer whirs and the card inches out, the only sound besides the wind, while the two sit, her head on his shoulder (35.0). 85.2-97.7: her head lifts (0.5); card from the slot (1.0); she looks at it (2.0); stands (1.0); to wall C (2.0); pins it with the spare pin (3.0); back to her chair and sits (2.0); head on his shoulder (1.0). 97.7-101.2: insert of PR-27 pinned between Jean's photos and PR-28 (3.5). 101.2-120.0: final wide (SH25), the wall above them and the two of them at the table below, the wind at the door (18.8).
- **emotional shift**: Before: her thumb rests on the icon (1.2 s), as it did twice in the car. Trigger, seen by the camera: she lifts her head, turns past the back of his head to the next man's two prints, and holds 1.0 s. After: she turns back, lays her head on his shoulder again and presses. The press is the change: her own face on her screen, eyes in the lens, smiling. Second beat, a hold: her fingers stop short of his chin, his gaze passes wide of the lens, and she takes the picture with no change of face.
- **transition in**: Match cut from SC17: seated in black in the white chair becomes seated in black under the wall, with the wind at the door.
- **transition out**: End of the pilot. Hard cut to black at the end of the final wide; the wind carries over the black.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' Her first press of the flip icon, and PR-27 pinned between Jean's wall and the next man's, carry it. Cut this and the viewer loses the answer to every thumb rest: she wanted one picture with him and gets it only when he can't look. They also lose the setup the series repeats.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Front-camera screen. Method: composite the UI; the print control is an original icon with no text, in the A10 package. Acceptance: the lantern lights her face, her eyes are in the lens, and his eyes read off to his left, past and below it.; Phone placement. Acceptance: in the front-camera frame both his eyes read, and they never meet the lens or look down at the screen. If a take shows his eyes meeting the lens, move the phone; never re-pose his head.; Pistol PR-02, draw and drop into the bag. Method: practical, a non-firing replica under an armorer who checks it before each take and holds it between takes (producer; pending). Acceptance: the muzzle points down through the draw, never crosses Jean, crew or the lens, and lies toward wall B in the bag, below the held frame. It never goes on the table.; Fingers toward his chin. Acceptance: they stop about 2 cm short; there is no contact and no nod. The head-alone rule holds.; Hair down. Acceptance: she is seated before the elastics come out; both go into her right front pocket, and the curls fall across his black shoulder before her head lands. No hair crosses the phone's lens or his eyes in the front-camera frame (stylist confirms VENUS_L03's fall).; Trigger look. Acceptance: it reads in the wide with the sound off.; Printer whir. Method: a practical unit, never sped up. Acceptance: the whir starts 1.0 s after her thumb taps print, so it has a visible cause. If the unit runs longer or shorter than 35.0 s, the final wide absorbs the difference and never runs under 15 s.; PR-27 insert, 3.5 s. Acceptance: the card-size print reads between Jean's grid and PR-28, clearly smaller than the 15 x 10 prints; PR-27 is pre-printed from the actual frame.; Final wide. Acceptance, tested after degrain: nothing moves but her breathing, then live grain and gate weave over the whole frame.; Dead eyes: the V01 makeup lock, no blink.; PR-28 shows a fictional man; the courthouse facade has no inscription or seal, and no real case is shown. No score.

The Venus actor's written consent must name V02's organic and paid social use and the pitch-deck embed, worldwide, with the term from first use. Status: required, pending. Owner: producer. |
| SND-V02-006 | V02 | foley + hard cut | V02-B06 / SH07, 11.0-13.0 s (f264-f312); thumb on f264-f271, rest f272-f300, back f301-f311; mic cut f312 (SH07 f48) | On top of the SND-V02-004 bed. | SND-V02-008 | V02 | music: none (by design), plus the sound arc and sync map | V02, 0.0-15.0 s (f0-f360) | No music and no temp track, as in SND-V01-016. V02 must read with the sound off, so the timer, the eyes, the thumb and the card carry it. The arc: the first sync sound is the stroke at f0 and the hook sync is the pinch at f29 (1.21 s). The loudest point is the roar at f36. The quietest stretch is f144-f216. The laugh runs against the eyes f216-f262, and digital silence runs from f312. No platform trending audio or library track may be added at posting; any added sound needs its own cue, source and scope. Owner for posting: producer. |
| SND-D01-004 | D01 | foley + atmos + production dialogue (decorum at the bar) | Pilot SC05-SC08, about 180-660 s (3:00-11:00); 'Have we met before?' at the top of SC05, about 180 s; first verdict in SC06, about 330-480 s; SC08 window from about 585 s; picture-led | We hear the game on the counter, and nothing scores it (A08: no jukebox, no bar music). VENUS: “Have we met before?” opens it over the near-empty room. VENUS: “How about I get this first round. Two Naked and Famous.” Two glasses land together. A straw laid flat in a wet ring makes a soft, tacky tick, a straw drawn back out makes a dry slide, and a glass is set down for every drink, so each lost straw has its own sound. SC06: VENUS: “A true martyr on a Thursday night, alone in an empty bar. Who slayed you, my friend?” JEAN: “She left. Packed while I was at work. I never once raised my voice.” Her glass goes down, then the wall straw slides dryly out, then JEAN: “That's a no?” VENUS: “That's a drink.” SC07: the chimney straw is pushed into the soft candle wax with a faint drag. SC08, through the window: nothing is heard from inside, only the street side, a far car and wind at the door frame. The laughs are seen, not heard, and the cuts land on them. The coupe rocking on the base straw is silent too. Inside at last call the room comes back. The glasses are cleared. JEAN's phone photo is taken with no shutter sound (A10). The chimney comes out of the wax with a tiny soft release and ticks once against the tin in her jacket pocket. The room is carried by a drinks-fridge compressor and a glass washer, and any background voices are unintelligible walla. Dialogue is recorded clean on set. Cut on the laughs (tone: genuine). | Source: foley recorded for this production, and production dialogue recorded on set with the Venus, Jean and Bartender actors, whose written consent for the pilot scope is required. Walla is recorded with consented background performers and kept unintelligible. 'Naked and Famous' is a spoken cocktail name; the producer's check is pending (A10). Scope: the pilot, as SND-D01-001. Status: pending. Owner: producer. |
| SND-D01-005 | D01 | foley + production dialogue (the tin and the last straw) | Pilot SC12-SC13, about 900-1200 s (15:00-20:00); 'Let's take a trip.' in SC12; lid opens at the end of SC13, about 1195 s (Act Two button); picture-led | The track (SND-D01-001) plays low from the bedroom under both scenes and is carved at 1-4 kHz under every line. SC12: ice drops into two glasses, a pour follows, and the glasses are squared side by side on the white lacquered table. Her hands go still on them on a bar-1 downbeat, as the track fills the room. JEAN: “Whew!” as he blows out the drink. Her glass is set down with the straw standing in its ice. The closed metal tin slides across to him in one glide of metal on lacquer, a low, even hiss. He turns it over in his palm, and nothing inside is heard. He tries the lid, and it doesn't give under her fingertip: one dry metal tick. VENUS: “Let's take a trip.” Between lines, never under a word, the ice settles around the standing straw. SC13: after JEAN: “That's for you to find out.” the straw comes out of the ice with a wet drag and is laid flat with a wet tick. Her fingertip lifts, the catch releases, and the lid swings open toward him: a dry hinge, then a small knock at its stop, placed in a bar-4 hole so nothing covers it. Hard cut on the knock; the track carries on into SC14. Nothing inside the tin is ever heard, so the method stays off screen. | Source: foley recorded for this production (a prop tin with a hinged lid, a white lacquered table, glass, ice, straw). The dialogue is covered by the Venus and Jean actors' pilot consent. Scope: the pilot, as SND-D01-001; owned by the production. Status: pending. Owner: producer. |
| SND-D01-001 | D01 | music, in scene (the trance-soul track) | Pilot SC11-SC16: enters about 870 s (SC11, through the bathroom door) and about 905 s (SC12, her dial); plays through SC16; grid downbeat 1381.8 s; dial turn 1391.4-1394.2 s; line 1476.0-1477.6 s (proposed); hard out at the switch-off, 1496.3 s; picture-led | The pilot's only music (A08). Venus plays it from the small white speaker on her nightstand to quiet herself; it is never score. It comes from a small mono speaker with little below about 120 Hz. Composer brief: 120 BPM in 4/4 (beat 0.5 s, bar 2.0 s), one four-bar loop of 8.0 s that repeats with no click, gap or tail at the join, written for the speaker's range. Bars 1-3: a soft four-on-the-floor kick whose attack carries the pulse, an off-beat bass pitched so its upper harmonics carry, electric-piano minor-ninth chords, a filtered pad and a low, wordless female vocal. Bar 4 (6.0-8.0 s of each loop) is the hole: kick, bass and piano drop out, leaving the pad with its filter closed down and one held low vocal note, so 1-4 kHz is nearly empty for 2.0 s. Entry, SC11: it starts low under the ice, heard through the bathroom door, the kick thumping through the wood. SC12 shows the same start from her side: the dial clicks on, and the loop begins low on a bar-1 downbeat. Through SC12-SC14 it plays on from the bedroom, carved under the lines. For SC15-SC16 the edit lays the loop so a bar-1 downbeat lands at 1381.8 s, 2.8 s after the rasp, and the staging counts 8.0 s loops from there (holes at 1379.8 s + 8n). Her dial turn, 1391.4-1394.2 s (SND-D01-002), lifts it from about 20% to about 80%. The next hole, 1395.8-1397.8 s, is the first where the pad and the held note sit over his breath. SC15's five-loop hold is 40.0 s on this grid. In SC16 every hole carries no breath. VENUS: “J. Stay for breakfast.” sits in one hole, proposed 1475.8-1477.8 s: 4 words, spoken 1476.0-1477.6 s, in about 1.6 s of the 2.0 s hole. Read: said to him, landing on 'Stay'; she is coaxing him up; not a plea, not grief. The music is laid in post, not played back on set, so the line records clean. The next hole, 1483.8-1485.8 s, is left empty: no answer. Exit: the switch-off click at 1496.3 s, 0.5 s into bar 2, stops the track hard mid-bar with no ring-out. Swap test: the hole is built for his breath, so on another film it lands on nothing. | Source: a commissioned original composition and master, with a wordless session vocalist. Scope: the pilot in all media, worldwide, in perpetuity, plus trailer, pitch and promo use of the pilot. Required: the composer's originality warranty and a similarity check, and the vocalist's written consent for that scope. No generated vocals or stems. The line is covered by the Venus actor's consent (SND-V01-015). Status: pending. Owner: producer. Not used in V01 or V02. |
| SND-D01-002 | D01 | voice, non-verbal + foley (Jean's breathing; the dial) | Pilot SC15, about 1290-1440 s (21:30-24:00); empty hole 1371.8-1373.8 s; rasp about 1379 s, placed at 1379.8 s; dial turn 1391.4-1394.2 s; picture-led | His breathing is Jean's last sound: slow and loud, face-down, muffled by the pillow, and clearest in the track's bar-4 holes at low volume. On her side of the room, his black clothes are folded square on the white chair (cotton on cotton). In the bathroom the tap runs and her hands wash at the basin, and his breathing carries through the open door over the water. The cabinet stays shut: no catch and no pills, because SC15 shows no meds. Back in bed his breathing stops. The hole at 1371.8-1373.8 s carries only the pad and the held note, the quietest point of the night. Then his breath catches with one rasp on the inhale, at the head of the next hole (about 1379 s in the treatment; placed at 1379.8 s so nothing masks it). No gurgle and no death-rattle cliché. He breathes on, ragged, in the holes after it. Her hand passes the phone on the nightstand without touching it and turns the speaker's dial (the PR-54 volume ring) in one continuous rotation, 1391.4-1394.2 s: a soft wool rub over a faint felt-and-plastic detent. The track rises in step with her hand until his breathing sits under the pad. | Source: recorded for this production with the actor cast as Jean (not yet cast). His written consent is required for his breathing and the rasp, in the pilot scope (as SND-D01-001). No library or synthetic breath. The foley, including the dial on the PR-54 prop speaker, is owned by the production. Status: required, pending. Owner: producer. |
| SND-D01-006 | D01 | foley + room tone (the clean-up) | Pilot SC16 after the switch-off to SC17, about 1496.3-1680 s; fitted corner about 1500 s; silent service-bay insert about 1530-1534 s; picture-led | No music. After the switch-off at 1496.3 s the bedroom is room tone only. She sits up, and the fitted sheet's elastic snaps off her corner, the first sound after the music. SC17's 4 s service-bay insert is silent, as written: no events, no street through the roller door, no lift hum, only the faintest enclosed air so it doesn't read as a dropout. Room tone comes in hard on the cut upstairs: sealed and white, with a faint air-vent hiss. Absent: the street, neighbors and any clock. Dressing him: black cotton drawn over his arms, the white armchair's cushion sighing under his weight, and the acetate hinge of his sunglasses opened and slid on (the V01 acetate take, a plant for SC01). His canvas duffel is zipped in one pull and stood by the door. The bed: the rest of the sheets dragged off, a fresh sheet snapped open in the air, the corners pulled tight and tucked, her palms smoothing. The bathroom: the cabinet's catch, a cloth over the glass shelf, and the pill organizer lifted. Its full compartments shift once with a small dry rattle, and it is wiped under and set back square beside the amber vial, glass on glass. No label is read and no drug is named. The threshold: her knees on the hard floor, the cloth scrubbing the three drops in short hard strokes, two soft folds as the pink cloth goes square, then the bin's pedal and lid. The flat straw, dropped in on top of the cloth, lands with no sound. The tin's hinge clicks shut, and it goes into her pocket alone, with a rustle of fabric. Then the hold on the finished room with Jean in the white chair: room tone only, longer than is comfortable (tone: clean). Jean makes no sound. | Source: foley recorded for this production. The sunglasses come from the V01 acetate session. The room tones are recorded in a white-finished interior and an enclosed service bay (location releases required). Scope: the pilot, as SND-D01-001; owned by the production. Status: pending. Owner: producer. |
| SND-D01-003 | D01 | foley + sfx (the tag: the flip-camera press) | Pilot SC18, about 1680-1800 s (28:00-30:00); flip-camera press about 1750 s, the printer after it; picture-led | This follows straight on from V01, with the same room tone, the wind through the open doorway and the open door on its stop (SND-V01-012 and 013). An acetate tick as she unhooks his sunglasses from her neckline and sets them by the oranges. Paper comes out of her bag, then two push pins go into the wall for the new man's two prints. Her chair drags to his right (the V01 chair take). Cotton as her head settles on his right shoulder, then the phone is lifted. The focal sound: her thumb squeaks to the flip-camera icon and knocks in the phone body. These are the same takes as SND-V01-004 and 008, so the ear knows the gesture, and this time there is no slide back: the press completes, heard as a 2-frame room-tone dropout. Two of her fingers lift toward his chin and stop. There is no touch, so there is no sound. The photo is taken with no shutter sound (A10). Then the palm-sized printer whirs and feeds the paper out, the only sound in the room. One more pin as the print goes up between the two walls. Then room tone and wind, and the door knocks its stop once, as in V01. Proposal, for approval: no laugh here. Every laugh so far was aimed at him from behind the lens; in front of it she only smiles. | Source: foley recorded for this production. The thumb takes are reused from the SND-V01-004 and 008 sessions, and the chair, door and room tone from the V01 room session. The printer is recorded on a debadged unit. Scope: the pilot, as SND-D01-001; owned by the production. No manufacturer UI sounds (A10). Status: pending. Owner: producer. |

| V01-SH12 | 34.8s | 120f @24 | Full 16:9 MWS from a virtual camera ahead of the car, 4.5 m from her and 10° west of her straight-ahead line (4.43 m north and 0.78 m west of her, 46 cm outboard of the driver side), heading world 167°, looking back south. The lens is at 240 cm, tilted down 17°. Her eyeline to the lens crosses the header plane at 133 cm, 15 cm over the 118 cm header. HEADER: a hard line across the car about 54% down. It meets Venus at her collar (about 92 cm) and Jean at his (about 88 cm), so both heads and collars stand in open air above it. GLASS: 54-69% down. ### Every shot in full

#### V01-SH01

- **scene id**: SC01
- **deliverable ids**: V01
- **purpose**: V01-B01 (0-4 s): his head is turned to the door, and her laugh shakes the picture · must-have. Her shadow on him is the only trace of her in her own footage (R2). It lands on him only under the declared 260° sun-azimuth cheat (true 248°; the derivation is in assumptions). Method: the registered Jean element (blocker 3). The UI is composited in finishing.
- **duration frames**: 96
- **fps**: 24
- **character ids**: JEAN; VENUS
- **look ids**: JEAN_L02; VENUS_L03
- **location id**: LOC-01
- **prop ids**: PR-01; PR-04; PR-56; PR-57; PR-11
- **framing**: FLOOR PLAN (LOC-01 driving north through LOC-02; car cm, x rearward from the windscreen base, y from the driver door). The axis is the road. Pos 1: Venus in the driver seat at y 32. Her skull spans car-x 88-106 (centre 97), eye 110 at x 90, crown about 121, and the low puff at her nape is flattened against the front face of her own pad at x 108. Pos 2: Jean in P1 at y 93, torso square, head turned 90° right, right cheek on the pad (x 108, y 100, pad top 108), face east to his door, right forearm slack on the door top (72). His near (left) side is 61 cm east of her head: temple x 94 (104-110 cm), ear x 93-96 (96-102 cm), jaw angle and mole x 95 (90-93 cm), collar points x 91-97 (80-86 cm), back of skull and nape x 97-108. There are no practicals. Every V01 film setup stays on or west of the centreline (y 77.5). PHONE: PR-01 upright in her right hand, wrist on the dash arc (x 25, y 55), lens at 100 cm, aimed at world 152° across his left shoulder, tilted down 5°. PICTURE: the upright 9:16 phone frame at full height, centred, 31.6% of the width, between #0A0A0A pillars that carry FL-BLACK's live 35mm grain (SH01-SH11 and SH17), so the 16:9 frame reads as one film print holding a phone picture. At his 0.85 m plane: curl tips 8% down, ear 35%, jaw 47%, mole 52%, collar points 63%; across, brow 30%, ear 58%, nape 85%. Her shadow covers car-x 77-97 at his plane: temple, ear, jaw, mole and collar points. Its rear edge crosses his head just behind the ear, about 73% across, and its top edge crosses his temple at 109 cm (24% down). Sunlit storefronts slide from frame-left to frame-right behind him. SH02-SH11 register to this frame.
- **lens intent**: Phone rear camera, 26mm FF-eq at 0.85 m with a 2x digital crop (52mm-eq; field about 0.35 x 0.62 m). This in-world lens sits outside the house set on purpose. Deep focus: collar, jaw and storefronts are all sharp, a little soft and noisy from the crop.
- **camera movement**: Handheld by her, wrist braced on the dash. Her laugh jolts the frame at 1.8 s with rolling-shutter skew, and the frame resettles on the same framing by 4.0 s. The fast auto shutter gives crisp, staccato frames.
- **lighting**: FL-PHONE. DECLARED CHEAT: the sun's azimuth is set to 260° (true 248°). Its 11° height and its colour, about 4000K against an AWB of 5000K, stay true. It comes from frame-right, 18° behind the lens plane. At the true 248° her shadow would land at car-x 63-83, 10 cm ahead of his ear, on the air in front of his face; no frame of SH01-SH08 shows the sun or a horizon it could be checked against. Her head and shoulders throw one shadow across his temple, ear, jaw and collar (85-109 cm, car-x 77-97 at his plane), and her pad's shadow takes his nape below about 96 cm. Gold holds only his crown and curl tips above 109 cm, the back of his skull behind x 97 and his pad's rear rim, 6:1. Sky (10000K) fills the shade, so the shadow reads blue on black. Separation is by value: his shaded head sits against stucco 2-3 stops brighter. The sky clips flat, and the fingerprint smear hazes the brightest stucco. Film layer: grain only.
- **action**: 0-1.8 s: hold, while his crown curls lift and settle as one mass. 1.8-3.4 s: laugh jolts. The shadow's top edge bobs 3-5 cm across his temple, and its rear edge slides 3-5 cm fore and aft over his ear and the back of his skull, flashing gold there. 3.4-4.0 s: the frame settles.
- **performance**: Jean: no breath, no blink; only the wind moves him. Venus, off picture: laughing at a lover who sleeps through the best part.
- **board note**: We see him through her phone: a black collar and jaw against gold stucco, with her shadow lying across him. At f43 her laugh jolts the frame and her shadow bobs over his temple. He doesn't move, and that is the first wrong note. The mole on his LEFT neck reads (a mole on the right means a mirrored frame). Cut: straight, a time jump (the timer leaps six minutes) to the stop sign, which is new information.
- **on screen text**: REC 00:00:04
- **sound cues**: SND-V01-001 from f0 (phone-mic wind, engine, tyres; awning f20); SND-V01-015 laugh f43-f70 (the jolts run to f81: flag 2); SND-V01-016 from f0 (no score)
- **entry state**: VENUS: off picture, driving; PR-01 upright in her right hand, wrist on the dash arc; left hand on the wheel; her shadow on his collar and jaw. JEAN (JEAN_L02): passenger seat, cheek on the headrest, head to the door; top button fastened; PR-04 on; collar C1; mole on the LEFT neck; curls moving. PR-01: recording, rear 2x, LED off. Car moving north through the town.
- **exit state**: VENUS: off picture, laugh over, frame settled; PR-01 in her right hand, wrist on the arc; left hand on the wheel. JEAN: head to the door, unmoved; top button fastened; PR-04 on; collar C1. PR-01: recording, rear 2x, LED off. Car moving north.
- **generation risk**: medium: Jean must not breathe, blink or nod, and the moving shadow must not move his head. Method: one registered pose with sun and shade passes, blended through a moving shadow matte, plus one shared curl layer. Reject mirrored takes (mole on the right). The REC UI is composited, never generated. Phone look: reject halation, bloom or shallow depth; the sky clips flat white; film grain only, added in finishing. Pending: B1 (laugh), B5 (phone UI).

#### V01-SH02

- **scene id**: SC01
- **deliverable ids**: V01
- **purpose**: V01-B02 (4.0-8.9 s): at a stop sign nobody else will ever reach, her hand brushes his collar clean · must-have; source of V02-SH01. Collar C1 to C2. PR-10 stands at its world-bible mark, spun to 325° and square to this lens. Method: three layers under one sun at the declared 260° cheat (true 249°), 10° high, 3900K. (1) A practical foreground on the LOC-01 buck (rig 3a): the hand double, seated with her skull at car-x 88-106, casts her own shadow and works SH-3 on a head-and-shoulders form in P1. (2) Jean above the collar band is the registered element with a 10° sun pass, so nothing above the band moves under her touch. (3) A generated background under the same cheated sun, with 'STOP' composited if the letters distort. Risk: extra fingers, or a nod. Fallback: her hand rests on his collar.
- **duration frames**: 118
- **fps**: 24
- **character ids**: JEAN; VENUS
- **look ids**: JEAN_L02; VENUS_L03
- **location id**: LOC-01
- **prop ids**: PR-01; PR-10; PR-58; PR-56; PR-57; PR-04
- **framing**: Registered to SH01. The front bumper stops at x 3,096 with PR-58 just ahead. PR-10 (world x 3,088; 5.1 m south and 3.8 m east of his head; its face 2.1-2.85 m up) sits 7° to the door side of his head and 11° above it: the octagon spans about 5-30% across and 2-17% down, its top tip under the 7% bar, with 'STOP' about 8-11% down beyond his curls. Her left hand enters from frame-right about 75% down, under her own right forearm, and works the collar points at 48-64% down, 38-58% across, fingers below the jaw. Her forearm leaves frame-right at 80-88% down. Her shadow's top edge sits at 110 cm (23% down) and climbs to about 113 cm (18%) as she leans in; its rear edge holds just behind his ear.
- **lens intent**: Phone 26mm FF-eq at 0.85 m, 2x digital (52mm-eq), matching SH01 and SH04-SH06, so V02's cut from B01 to B02 lands on one frame of him. Deep focus: collar, hand, jaw and the sign at 7.3 m are all sharp.
- **camera movement**: Handheld, wrist on the arc. The frame dips for 0.8 s as the car stops, then holds the registered frame.
- **lighting**: FL-PHONE. The sun at the declared 260° (true 249°), 10° high, 3900K on an AWB of 5000K, from frame-right 18° behind the lens. Her hand works inside her own shadow (car-x 77-97 at his plane), so hand and collar read as one cool plane. Gold holds his crown, curl tips and the back of his skull, 6:1. The sign's chalked face, 65° off the cheated sun, reads 1 stop under the gold on his crown. Grain only.
- **action**: f0-f18: the car stops and the frame dips; the wind drops and his curls settle within about 12 frames. f19-f35: her hand enters. f36-f64: two strokes take the collar points from dusted to black. f65-f88: she pinches the left point flat. f89-f107: her hand rests. f108-f117: as it withdraws, it crosses the lens 0.5 m out, a frame-filling blur that hides the join.
- **performance**: Unhurried and tender; her hand rests a beat before it leaves. Nothing above his collar band moves.
- **board note**: The octagon sits just past his brow at an empty crossroads. Her left hand enters under her own forearm, two strokes turn the collar points black, and a pinch presses one flat. She tends him like a lover, and nothing above his collar moves. First look at the crossroads, held 4.9 s. Cut: on action; her hand blurring across the lens hides the join into SH03.
- **on screen text**: REC 00:06:12

#### S01-SH01

- **purpose**: S01 key art, 2:3: Venus laughing at the wheel, her phone showing Jean as phone video, and the real Jean beside her, pale and still in golden light · must-have. Planned on option C, which Lucas chooses between (blocker 10): the southbound graded-track leg at REC about 00:12, sun 9° high at a true 249°, 3700K. Heading south in a left-hand-drive car, the low west sun lands on Jean's side, so no azimuth cheat is needed here. The sun sits within a few degrees of the lens plane on frame-right, so a side flag is named (see lighting). DECLARED VIEWPOINT CHANGE: the route and the stills copy ask for 'behind her right shoulder', but from there her face reads only as a cheek line and his profile hides behind the pad. The lens moves behind his right shoulder, outboard on his door side. There are two declared phone cheats (see framing). The screen is turned to 20° off our axis so the second Jean survives a deck thumbnail, and a chroma lock keeps the real Jean pale in full gold (its cost is logged in blocker 10). This breaks 'hands before faces' on purpose, because a poster needs its lead. LIMERENCE only, in the top 15%. His eyes and neck mole stay hidden to protect V01's reveal. The screen needs a one-off phone frame, because V01 never shows this leg.

- **fps**: 24

The lens is at car x 280, y 150 (5 cm inside his door line, 72.5 cm outboard of the centreline), 155 cm up, off eye level to see over the pads, heading world 155°, tilted down 12°. VENUS, 2.19 m out, sits about 30% across, eyes about 49% down. Her nose is at 272°, looking straight at him and held within 95° of the direction of travel (92°), which puts it 55° off the lens: a right three-quarter, tipped back laughing, with her far iris at the nose bridge. Her eyeline passes just under the phone's lower edge to him, never to the lens. JEAN, 1.8 m out, sits about 71% across in P1, facing 270° (his door, into the sun), 73° off his nose: a deep profile of brow, lens rim, nose tip, grey-ash lips and jaw. His temple-to-jaw plane clears the pad's west edge by at least 6 cm, his eyes stay behind the lens and the cheek line, the mole is on his far side, and his crown curls lift rearward. His forearm on the door top lies out of frame-right (continuity holds). The horizon is about 29% down: the graded track running south, scrub, hazy hills at frame-left, and dusk-gold sky in the top 15% for the title. PHONE CHEATS, declared. (1) PR-01 is in her right hand between their heads at car x 100, y 70, 115 cm, centred at about 52% across and 49% down. Truly aimed at him, its screen would face her (about 106°). It is turned about 110° to face world 356°, 20° off our axis, so 6.8 cm of its 7.2 cm width shows: about 5.2% of frame width and 7.7% of frame height. That is large enough to read as a second, upright Jean at deck-thumbnail size. (2) Its picture is a one-off frame taken from the dash arc.
- **lens intent**: 35mm FF-eq (25mm S35), a 2:3 extraction (16.5 x 24.8 mm on the S35 4-perf gate, camera rolled), focused at 1.95 m, T4: sharp from about 1.49 to 2.83 m, holding Jean (1.8 m), the screen (1.97 m) and her eyes (2.19 m). The desert is soft.
- **camera movement**: A single still frame.
- **lighting**: FL-GOLD (balance 5600K). The sun is at a true 249°, 9° high, 3700K, from frame-right. It sits 94° off the lens axis in azimuth, about 96° counting the 12° tilt and its height: within a few degrees of the lens plane. FLAG: a frame-right side flag (an eyebrow on the matte box's right edge, set on the sun line) keeps it off the front element and the matte-box edges. Acceptance: no flare, veil or sun streak from the frame-right edge. Jean faces 270°, 21° off the sun, which sits on his left: hard gold across the front planes of his profile (brow, lens rim, nose, grey-ash lips, jaw). His cheek against the pad falls to shade, filled by the sky (10000K) and bounce off the bone leather, 6:1. Venus's nose is at 272°, 23° off the sun, which is also on her left, while the lens sits 55° to her right. Gold falls across the front of her face and her far cheek, and her near cheek turns into the edge of the light (short lighting), filled by the leather and her white shirt, 3:1. Her hair edge halates softly. PALLOR LOCK, measured after the film pass on matched patches of each sunlit cheek: his CIELAB chroma is no more than half of hers. His lips and lids hold the grey-ash hue at C* 8 or under. No skin secondary, warm window or grade move touches him, and any warmth on her is keyed to her alone. The gold lights his shape, never his colour; his skin is never lifted. Separation: his lit profile against the silver bonnet beyond, which the 9° sun only grazes (1-1.5 stops under); her dark hair and halated edge against sunlit caliche. SCREEN: a plain lit panel carrying the dash-arc frame of his collar, left jaw, ear and sunglasses arm in cool shade with a thin gold rim, in the phone look, with the REC dot, no timer and no trace of her: two of him, none of her. It is keystoned to 20°, 1 stop under her hair rim and below the halation threshold. It faces 356°, 107° off the sun, so no sun reaches the glass; it reflects only the shaded rear deck behind the seats, held under the picture.
- **action**: She laughs, holding the phone on him. He is still.
- **performance**: Venus: the real laugh, in love, beside a dead man. Jean: absolute stillness.
- **board note**: At thumbnail size: her laughing three-quarter at left; the small lit phone at centre showing him under a red REC dot; and the real him at right, gold-edged, head to his door. Two of him, none of her. LIMERENCE sits in the top 15% of sky. This breaks 'hands before faces' on purpose, as decided upstream: a poster needs its lead.

### Contradictions the storyboard flagged

- 1 · V01-SH17, SH23, SH24, V02-SH07 · Sound plan vs camera plan (owner: sound designer). Root cause: these CP hits were spotted to the previous camera-plan cut, and the camera plan has since re-split SH16/SH17, reordered SH23 (printer first) and re-split V02-SH07. What breaks (global frames): SH17 thumb on at f1282-f1301 (knock f1301) and off at f1330-f1344, but the picture has on at f1272-f1290, off at f1320-f1333 and a thumbless hold at f1334-f1343, so every hit lands 10 frames late and the slide sounds over an empty frame. SH23 has oranges at f1805 and the printer at f1848, but the picture has the printer at f1752-f1773 and the oranges at f1810-f1821 (the jugs at f1822-f1843 fit). The chair drag runs f1874-f1908, from 4 frames inside SH23, but the picture drag is SH24 f0-f21 (f1878-f1899). V02-SH07 has the thumb on at f269-f278 (knock f278) and back at f298-f307, but the picture has on at f264-f271, rest at f272-f300 and back at f301-f311. Fix: re-spot to those camera-plan frames; no picture change. Boarded with the plan's frames and marked in each panel.
- 2 · V01-SH01, SH10, SH22, SH23, SH24 · Picture actions and a script sound with no hit (owner: sound designer). SH01: the laugh jolts run f43-f81 (1.8-3.4 s), but SND-V01-015's B01 laugh ends at f70. SH10 f58 (global f754): the road-bump jolt that hides the plate join has no hit in SND-V01-005. SH22 f78-f95 (global f1710-f1727): the two bags shrugged off have no thud in SND-V01-011 (where they land is flag 3). SH23: script B12's 'cans set down on wood' has no hit, although the six cans go down in three pairs at f46-f81 (global f1774-f1809); the paper sacks sliding off the table at f135-f149 are also unsounded. SH24 f60-f89: her walk past the lens on bare boards has no footsteps. Fix: extend the B01 laugh to f81, and add a tyre thud and handling bump at SH10 f58, a bag thud at SH22, three paired can knocks, a paper slide, and boots on boards. Boarded without them and marked.
- 3 · V01-SH22 exit to SH26, and S02 · Camera plan vs world bible (owners: cinematographer and production designer). The camera plan's SH22 has 'Just past the jamb she shrugs the bags off' (f78-f95), and SH23 opens on the sacks going onto the table, so no frames carry PR-05 or PR-06 anywhere else. The world bible puts PR-05 S7 at x 40-90, y 20-48 under the boarded window and PR-06 S2 at x 95-140, y 20-45: 1.1-2.1 m west of the jamb (x 250). Dropped at the jamb, they sit at or in front of the SH25/SH26/S02 lens (x 280, y -10), under the tripod or in the held frame's floor band. The last version moved them to the window without a flag; that quiet fix is undone. Boarded at the camera plan's drop point, just past the jamb, with no picture change. Fix: either SH22 ends with her walking the bags to the window, out of the doorway view (cinematographer), or the bible moves the bags' mark to the jamb side, clear of SH25's frame and lens (production designer); SC18's PR-06 S3 (PR-28 taken out of it) then follows the chosen mark.
- Checks run: 37 panels, one per camera-plan shot, in its order (V01-SH01 to SH26, V02-SH01 to SH08, S01, S02, S03). Frame totals: V01 2160 f = 90 s, V02 360 f = 15 s; every cue frame was converted to shot-local from those cumulative counts. Cards and lines were checked word for word against the script: no spoken lines (A15); REC cards per beat (SH09-SH10 01:04:29, SH11 01:04:37, SH17 01:04:55, V02-SH01 00:06:13); LIMERENCE only on V01-SH26, V02-SH08 and S01. Every cue ID exists in the sound plan. CP hits were compared with the camera-plan action (flags 1 and 2); T hits are boarded on the picture per SND-V01-017, including the SH02 pull-away, now at SH03 f0 on the lurch. Entry equals the previous exit everywhere (SH02 to SH03 and SH15 to SH16 re-checked) except at marked scene changes, time jumps and elisions: V01-SH02, SH04-SH07, SH09, SH18, SH20, SH21, SH22 (walk), SH23 (scene change and walk), SH24 (walk and drag start); V02-SH02, SH03, SH04 (1.6 s), SH05. Prop marks were checked against the world bible; the only mismatch is the SH22 bag drop (flag 3). Collar states were checked against PR-57 and the REC clock: S01 at about 00:12 is C2. Cut labels were checked against the timer: SH06 to SH07 is a 37 s jump cut. First looks of 1 s or more: V01-SH02 4.9 s, SH12 5 s, SH15 3.6 s, SH18 1.25 s, SH20 4 s, SH22 4 s, SH23 5.2 s; V02-SH01 1.5 s, SH03 and SH04 1.0 s. V02 sound-off caption check: passed, with no caption needed and no copywriter flag. The timer jump (00:06:13 to 00:52:30) carries the hour, the unblinking eyes carry the death, the thumb resting on and leaving the flip icon carries the route idea, and the cut to black with the REC dot gone carries the stop. Only the laugh is lost muted, and SND-V02-005 accepts the frame shake carrying it. The 35mm and phone-look reject rules are on every panel. Must-avoid: no VO, slow motion, gore, nudity or brands; legible text is limited to STOP, MOTEL, OFFICE, the REC timers and LIMERENCE. Verified facts shown: the gun in her back pocket (SH19-SH24) and the wall with none of them together (SH23-SH26, S02). No claim problem and no clash with Lucas's notes. Rights, consents, sign-offs and Lucas's S01 choice are listed as pending blockers B1-B7 and cited in each affected panel's risk.
