### Not supported yet

- V01-SH01-JEAN-SHADE (V01-SH01): ASPECT: 576x1024 crop. One reference. Gate 1. The grade replaces the two relight passes (12 images) because the profile has no registered relight.
- V01-SH01-IMG-BG (V01-SH01): ASPECT fallback. No cast, no reference.
- V01-SH02-IMG-BG (V01-SH02): ASPECT fallback. No reference.
- V01-SH04-IMG-BG (V01-SH04): ASPECT fallback.
- V01-SH05-IMG-BG (V01-SH05): ASPECT fallback.
- V01-SH06-IMG-BG (V01-SH06): ASPECT fallback.
- V01-SH07-IMG-BG (V01-SH07): ASPECT fallback.
- V01-SH09-IMG (V01-SH09): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH10-IMG (V01-SH10): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH12-IMG-CAR (V01-SH12): ASPECT fallback. Triage: two people 1 + motion 1 = 2; high per storyboard for identity, so three separate plates.
- V01-SH12-IMG-V (V01-SH12): ASPECT fallback. One reference; the car comes from the base, not from this generation.
- V01-SH12-IMG-J (V01-SH12): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH13-IMG (V01-SH13): ASPECT fallback. He is only a soft shape, so bleed is low; if his head drifts, matte it from SH12-IMG-J.
- V01-SH14-IMG (V01-SH14): ASPECT fallback. Her only; his head, the glasses and her right arm come from SH14-PRAC, so no two-person bleed. Gate 1.
- V01-SH15-IMG (V01-SH15): ASPECT fallback. One reference; her fingers are words, checked at visual lock. Gate 1.
- V01-SH15-IMG-ROAD (V01-SH15): ASPECT fallback. No reference.
- V01-SH16-IMG-CAR (V01-SH16): ASPECT fallback. New job for the review note: SH16's person plates now register to one car.
- V01-SH16-IMG-J (V01-SH16): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH16-IMG-V (V01-SH16): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH17-IMG (V01-SH17): ASPECT fallback: the 576x1024 crop needs a 1.25x upscale for a 720x1280 clip.
- V01-SH18-IMG (V01-SH18): ASPECT fallback. Sends the motel. Two people but tiny and faceless, so bleed is low; if either reads as a face, matte them from the SH21 elements.
- V01-SH20-IMG (V01-SH20): ASPECT fallback. Sends her back view; the office is words. 119 words with the look sentence.
- V01-SH21-IMG (V01-SH21): ASPECT fallback. Sends the car; she is words, face away. Jean is plated separately, so no bleed into his face.
- V01-SH21-IMG-J (V01-SH21): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH22-IMG (V01-SH22): ASPECT fallback. Sends her back view; door and path are words. 119 words with the look sentence.
- V01-SH23-IMG (V01-SH23): ASPECT fallback. No reference, so no face leaks into the cards.
- V01-SH24-IMG-BG (V01-SH24): ASPECT fallback. One reference for room continuity.
- V01-SH25-IMG (V01-SH25): ASPECT fallback. One reference. Counts are never asked of the generator.
- V02-SH03-IMG (V02-SH03): ASPECT fallback. Regenerated because a 720p crop would be 405 px wide; reverts to crops of the V01 plates once a 1080p or 4K adapter or upscaler is bound.
- V02-SH04-IMG (V02-SH04): ASPECT fallback. Reverts to crops of the V01 plates once a 1080p or 4K adapter or upscaler is bound.
- S01-IMG-CAR (S01-SH01): ASPECT fallback: 683x1024 crop; poster master needs the missing upscaler. Waits on Lucas's light choice (B6).
- S01-IMG-V (S01-SH01): ASPECT fallback. Triage: two people 1 + screen 2 = 3; plated separately. Breaks 'hands before faces' on purpose, as decided upstream.
- S01-IMG-J (S01-SH01): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- S01-IMG-SCREEN (S01-SH01): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- S02-COMP (S02-SH01): The master size needs an upscaler the profile lacks.
- Missing capability: Image aspects other than square (16:9, 9:16, 2:3, 4:5) at clip-input or master size
- Missing capability: An image upscaler for clip inputs and the S01 and S02 masters
- Missing capability: Multi-reference input (a character plus a car or location in one job)
- Missing capability: Video clips longer than 5 s
- Missing capability: End-frame (first and last frame) conditioning, for the chained clips and the SH15 and V02-SH04 turns
- Missing capability: Motion conditioning (driving a generated limb from a practical plate's path), which is why SH14 is practical from the shoulder down
- Missing capability: Video output above 720p, or a video upscaler, for the V01 master and the 1080x1920 V02 frames
- Missing capability: Pixel-registered relighting (same geometry, new light); replaced by a grade of the shade pass

### Every job in full

#### K01-CHR-JEAN-DEAD · V01-SH01

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image kit sheet, JEAN_L02 dead state -> CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1
- **prompt**: Character reference sheet on a plain mid-grey backdrop: one man of about forty in three views, front, left profile and from just behind his left shoulder. Broad shoulders, thick neck, waist thickening over the belt, short wide face, square jaw, heavy low brows, close beard with a hard cheek line. Dense tight black curls about five centimetres high on top as they sit, picked out loose at the crown, sides tapered short. Deep brown skin washed grey-ash at the lips and lids, a small dark mole on the left side of his neck below the ear. Blue-black long-sleeve shirt buttoned to the top. Eyes open, fixed past the camera. Flat even light, everything sharp.
- **acceptance checks**: Same face in all three views, mole on his LEFT neck in each (reject any mirrored view); Top button fastened; top curls about 5 cm as they sit, loose at the crown for the A16 wind test; skin grey-ash, never lightened
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Square sheet, no reference, neutral light, no look sentence. Gate 1. Locks only after the B7 likeness check (consent blocker 3).
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K03-HND-VENUS · V01-SH02

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image kit sheet, settled hand anchors -> HND-VENUS@v1
- **prompt**: Hand reference sheet on a plain mid-grey card: a woman's right and left hands, backs up and then palms up. Long fingers, prominent knuckles, medium-deep brown skin with a warm undertone, bare short nails filed square. Right hand: a pale two-centimetre scar across the back between the thumb and index knuckles, and a pale six-millimetre nick across the back of the thumb at its first joint. Left hand: a small glossy pale oval burn on the back of the index finger just above the knuckle. Wrists bare, white cuffs rolled well above them. Soft even light, everything sharp.
- **acceptance checks**: Five fingers on each hand; bare nails, bare fingers; Right hand: scar and thumb nick. Left hand: only the index burn. Reject any mirrored placement
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Fits. The left-index burn is a proposal; if cut, round 2 regenerates the left hand bare. The hand double for SH02, SH07, SH14, SH17, SH23, SH24 and S03 is cast against this sheet.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K02-CAR · V01-SH12

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image kit sheet, LOC-01 car -> CAR-01@v1
- **prompt**: Vehicle reference sheet of a vintage open two-seater roadster with a 1950s shape, in three views: three-quarter front, side and three-quarter rear. Long bonnet, rounded wings, low cut-down doors, a plain oval grille with six horizontal bars, round lamps, plain hubcaps and smooth unbadged panels. Silver-grey metallic paint, bone leather seats and headrest pads, black steering wheel, driver seat on the left, top down. Parked on pale gravel in flat overcast light, everything sharp, the rear plate plain and unmarked.
- **acceptance checks**: No badge, script or grille shape from a real make; plate plain; Six-bar oval grille, bone headrests and left-hand drive in every view
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Fits. The one reference for the car bases SH12-IMG-CAR, SH16-IMG-CAR, SH21-IMG and S01-IMG-CAR.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K04-CHR-VENUS · V01-SH12

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image kit sheet, VENUS_L03 with a full-length back view -> CHR-VENUS@v1
- **prompt**: Character reference sheet on a plain mid-grey backdrop showing one woman in her mid-thirties in four views: front, right three-quarter and left profile from the waist up, plus one full-length view from behind. Long face, narrow jaw, heavy straight brows with the right one slightly higher, three small dark freckles on the left cheekbone, open pores, fine laugh lines, medium-deep brown skin with a warm undertone, bare of makeup. Dense dark brown curls with honey highlights in one low puff at the nape. White tucked button shirt, top two buttons open, sleeves rolled twice. Pale-blue high-rise bootcut jeans, brown-black belt and ankle boots. Flat even light, everything sharp.
- **acceptance checks**: Right brow higher and three freckles on the left cheek in every face view; reads 30+, pores and laugh lines kept; Back view: low puff, tucked white shirt, pale-blue bootcut jeans, brown-black belt and boots
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Fits. The back view carries SH19-SH22, where her face is away. Gate 1. Locks only after the B7 likeness check (consent blocker 3).
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K05-JEAN-EYES · V01-SH15

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image state reference, dead eyes -> JEAN-EYES@v1
- **prompt**: State reference: a close view of the same man's face in three-quarter from his left, eyes open, both upper lids resting exactly at the top edge of the dark brown irises. The corneas are dry and matte under a dull grey film. Fine gold dust sits on the lashes, in the lid creases and in the forehead lines; the lips are dry and grey-ash. Broad soft light from frame-right, plain dark backdrop, everything sharp.
- **reference asset ids**: CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1
- **acceptance checks**: No catchlight or wet shine; both lids at the top of the iris; Far iris clear of the nose bridge; same face as CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Square, one reference. Gate 1.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K06-MOTEL · V01-SH18

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image kit view, LOC-03 -> LOC03-MOTEL@v1
- **prompt**: Abandoned 1950s roadside motel in flat desert at dusk: a two-storey bleached stucco room block with a gutted upper floor, a single-storey office with a boarded door, a long corrugated carport on steel posts, and three dry palms. A tall sign on steel legs carries a peeling cream box and a faded red arrow panel, both faces plain and unmarked. Empty highway frontage, gravel forecourt, soft last light from the west, pale blue-grey sky, everything sharp.
- **acceptance checks**: Sign faces plain for composited letters; the site empty of vehicles and people; Office left of the carport, room block behind, as the LOC-03 plan
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Square location reference.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K08-CHR-JEAN-ALIVE · V01-SH23

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image kit sheet, JEAN_L01 living state -> CHR-JEAN-ALIVE@v1
- **prompt**: Character reference sheet on a plain mid-grey backdrop: the same man alive, in three views, front, left three-quarter and left profile. Deep brown skin in full warm colour with forehead shine, visible pores and puffy under-eyes. Eyes wet and clear with a normal catchlight, lids relaxed. Blue-black long-sleeve shirt with the top button open and the second fastened, and black sunglasses hooked by one arm into the placket over the second button, lenses outside at mid-chest. Flat even light, everything sharp.
- **reference asset ids**: CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1
- **acceptance checks**: Living skin tone, no grey-ash at lips or lids; top button open; Same face, curls and left-neck mole as CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1; wet eyes with a normal catchlight
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Fits. The only reference is the dead state, so the prompt names the living change. Placket glasses per the style bible (see blocker).
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K07-PRINT-01 · V01-SH23

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image single print, square cropped to 3:2 -> PRINT-JEAN-01@v1; two further crops in finishing, never flipped (a flip mirrors the mole)
- **prompt**: A single candid photograph taken with a long lens from across a night street: through a bar's plain front window, a man in all black sits alone at the counter, shoulders rounded over a short glass, three-quarter from his left, small in the frame. Warm amber light inside, cool streetlight reflections across the glass, the window and signs plain and unmarked. Landscape frame.
- **reference asset ids**: CHR-JEAN-ALIVE@v1
- **acceptance checks**: One photograph of one man alone, no other person, no text or numerals (verified fact); Living skin, no grey-ash; top button open; same face as CHR-JEAN-ALIVE@v1

- **prompt**: Upright phone-video frame, close from just behind and left of a man in a car's passenger seat: his black collar, left jaw, ear, the left arm of black sunglasses and a small dark mole on his neck, head turned away to his door, right cheek on a pale bone headrest pad. Curl tips near the top edge, ear a third down, jaw near the middle, collar points below. Lit only by open blue sky shade. A fine pale-gold dust film on both shoulders and collar points. Plain even grey behind him. Phone-video look, daylight: deep focus with everything sharp, hard-clipped highlights, slight digital softness, short-shutter crispness, clean grain-free frame.
- **reference asset ids**: CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1
- **acceptance checks**: Mole on his LEFT neck; only jaw and ear show (reject mirrored); Bands: curl tips 8%, ear 35%, jaw 47%, collar points 63% down; collar C1; The sun grade through the matte reads as low gold sun at 6:1, not a flat lift; if not, one relit pass goes to the cinematographer
- **capability supported**: false
- **capability notes**: ASPECT: 576x1024 crop. One reference. Gate 1. The grade replaces the two relight passes (12 images) because the profile has no registered relight.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

## Integrity check

Recommendation: revise.

| Severity | Where | Issue | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| major | V01-SH15 (45.0-46.4 s); V02-SH04 (5.0-6.0 s) | The generation prompt for V01-SH15-VID-A reads 'Head and shoulders turn as one block through about sixty degrees', and V02-SH04-VID reads 'Head and shoulders turn as one block through about forty-three degrees'. Both contradict the approved action. The treatment says 'her two fingers turn his head alone on the neck 60°... His torso stays square, and the collar moves only where the jaw drags it', and its acceptance requires 'torso and shoulders unmoved'. The camera plan follows the treatment (SH15; assumption (b) 'I followed the treatment'). Neither job's acceptance checks test the shoulders, so a block turn would pass review and break the 'torso square' continuity into SH16, SH18 and SC18. Fix: rewrite both prompts as a head-only turn on the neck with the torso and shoulders fixed, and add a difference-matte acceptance check on the shoulders and torso. Hold both jobs until Q02 settles the rule. | generation_supervisor |
| major | V01-B07/SH15; SC17; SC18 | The casting bible's JEAN performance_style says 'From the first frame of the drive his neck is fixed... his head and shoulders move only together, as one block... her two fingers turn the block... The shoulders rotate'. This contradicts the treatment's rule ('his head moves only when her hand moves it... a head-only turn on the neck, torso square'), which the camera plan follows. Treatment blocker 2 and camera plan blocker 6 are still open, and the generation plan has picked up the casting version (Q01). Fix: restate the body rule as the director's performance rule and make V01-B07 a head-only turn, with no rigor rationale. | casting_director |
| major | V01-SH12 (34.8-39.8 s, f835-f955) | The treatment's acceptance reads 'Laugh sync (B06, the only sync in V01)... laugh peaks align with the torso shakes within 2 frames', and B06 covers SH11-SH13. SND-V01-015 records the laugh 'while watching the generated SH13 picture' and calls SH13 'the only sync shot', and the storyboard's SH12 risk lists 'B1 (off-sync laugh)'. But SH12 shows her first face, laughing, with visible torso shakes for 5 s (generation job V01-SH12-VID-V: 'Her torso shakes with a belly laugh in pulses across the clip'). An unsynced laugh on the hero face reveal will read as dubbed. Fix: record and spot the one continuous laugh against the SH12 and SH13 picture together, with peaks within 2 frames of every visible shake from f835 to f1007. | sound_designer |
| major | V01-SH01, SH10, SH17, SH22, SH23, SH24; V02-SH03, SH04, SH07 | The hits were spotted to an earlier camera-plan cut (storyboard flags 1 and 2, confirmed here). SND-V01-008: thumb on at f1282-f1301 and off at f1330-f1344; the picture has the thumb on at f1272-f1290 and off at f1320-f1333, so every hit is 10 frames late. SND-V01-012: printer at f1848 and oranges at f1805; the picture has the printer at f1752-f1773 and the oranges at f1810-f1821. The chair drag (f1874-f1908) starts 4 frames inside SH23; the picture drag is f1878-f1899. SND-V02-006: thumb on at f269-f278 and back at f298-f307; the picture has it on at f264-f271 and back at f301-f311. SND-V02-003 assumes V02-SH04 starts at V01 f1080 with a 1.2 s elision, but the camera plan locks V01 45.4-46.4 s, a 1.6 s elision, so the 'first frame of the chin turn' headrest creak lands 0.4 s into the turn. Missing hits: the cans going down (SH23 f46-f81), the bag drop (SH22 f78-f95), the road-bump jolt (SH10 f58) and the walk-out footsteps (SH24 f60-f89). The B01 laugh ends at f70, but the picture's jolts run to f81. Fix: re-spot to the current camera-plan frames and add the missing hits. | sound_designer |
| major | V01-SH23 to SH26 (72-90 s); S02 | The treatment's SC03 flag reads 'Generate or shoot about 40 unique long-lens images... tiled to 250 landscape prints'. The generation plan builds the wall from 'The wall: 12 single-print prompts, each with two crops (never flipped) = 36 variants over about 250 cells'. That is 12 distinct photographs, each appearing about 21 times. At S02 deck size, and in SH23's close lower rows, the repeats can read as a tiled texture and undercut 'months' of watching, the verified-fact centrepiece. Fix: raise the count to at least 40 distinct photographs (for example 40 prompts at one variation each, within the ceiling), or get the director's written acceptance of 12 with a repeat-distance acceptance check. | generation_supervisor |
| major | All V01 and V02 jobs; S01, S02 masters | The generation plan says 'No adapter is bound'. Its missing capabilities include 'Video output above 720p, or a video upscaler, for the V01 master and the 1080x1920 V02 frames' and 'An image upscaler for clip inputs and the S01 and S02 masters'. Non-square frames are crops of a 1024 px square (576x1024). The treatment's acceptances require V02 phone plates 'at 1080x1920 or better so V02 isn't an upscale' and the eyes plate 'at 4K equivalent'. As planned, these cannot be met, and every capability_supported flag is unsourced. Fix: bind an adapter with sourced capabilities and restate each job's output size, or get written sign-off from the cinematographer and director on 720p plus a named upscaler, with acceptance checks at the delivered size. | generation_supervisor |
