### Sound

- Timing is in seconds from each deliverable's first frame at 24 fps, with frame = round(s x 24). CP marks the camera plan's frames as the package reviews relay them; the camera plan itself was not in this prompt. T marks a target to be spotted to the generated picture. Every hit is picture-led at ±2 unless marked sound-led.
- The cut into B03 at f240 comes from the script's beat boundary and still needs to be confirmed against the camera plan.
- The torso-shake frames in SH12 and SH13 were not relayed. The B06 laugh is recorded against SH12 and SH13 cut together and spotted to their shakes.
- From SH22 to the end the motel door stands open at 90° (world bible PR-15 S2, as relayed) and is behind camera from SH23 (treatment SC03). The knock when it opens, at f1704, is a target, and it plants the B13 knocks.
- The bag dropped in SH22 is taken to be his black canvas duffel (PR-05). If the storyboard drops her bag instead, keep the same frames with a lighter thud.
- The paper sacks set down in the dark (f1730-f1742) fill the unlit frames before the lantern. The script's 'cans set down on wood' is now carried by the three paired can knocks at f1774-f1809.
- V02-SH07 starts at f264, on the thumb onset, so the mic cut at f312 is SH07 f48. V02's thumb pass is quicker than V01's, so its foley is re-cut rather than lifted at V01's speed.
- D01: the script has the track playing from SC11/SC12 through SC16 ('The track still plays low' in SC15). I read the treatment's 'track entry 1381.8 s' as the bar-1 downbeat its SC15-SC16 loop count starts from, not as the track's first entrance. If the treatment means a true first entrance, the director and the copywriter must reconcile it with SC11-SC15 before the music is laid. The loop is unchanged either way.
- The rasp (about 1379 s in the treatment) is placed at 1379.8 s, the head of the hole just before that downbeat. The hole proposed for the line (1475.8-1477.8 s) is only a proposal; any SC16 hole on the grid before the switch-off works.
- The switch-off at 1496.3 s lands 0.5 s into bar 2 of a loop, so the track stops mid-bar as designed.
- The script's speaker 'dial' is the world bible's PR-54 volume ring, as relayed.
- 120 BPM, the 8.0 s loop and the 2.0 s hole follow the commissioner's note. The loop design is a proposal for the commission, not a spec from Lucas.
- SC15 has no cabinet or meds sound, because the script keeps the cabinet shut. The meds are heard only in SC17's clean-up, as one rattle from the full organizer, and are never named.
- SC17's service-bay insert is played near-silent because the script writes 'Four silent seconds'.
- US vocabulary (trunk, tires, curb, organizer) follows the script's decision.
- The 35mm look adds no sound. The switch from phone to film is carried by a change of mic perspective at f835.
- V01 and V02 have no spoken lines (A15). The laugh is the only voice, and Jean makes no sound. Native clip audio is muted, and no generated audio is used.
- V02-B01's 'wind on the mic' is a light-breeze take from the stop-sign session, because the driving wind is gone at the stop.
- The picture car is generated, and its sound is recorded on a hired vintage open two-seater. That such a car can be hired is a hypothesis, and no make is identified.
- The D01 cues are pilot anchors, not full coverage. Their times follow the outline and the treatment and move with the pilot edit.
- The tin has a hinged lid (production design to confirm). Nothing inside it is ever heard.
- Gun-handling foley uses a non-firing replica (production safety to confirm).
- The 2-frame dropout at the front-camera flip is a design choice, not a claim about how any phone behaves.
- Having no laugh in the tag is a proposal awaiting approval.
- Loudness and delivery specs are left to the mix.
- The Venus, Jean and Bartender actors are not yet cast, so every voice consent stays pending.
- Taste notes followed: sound design before music (no score in V01 or V02); restraint over explanation (no stings on the eyes, gun, tin, light or title); hands before faces (hand foley leads each beat); dry over sentimental. Bent on purpose, as the script flags: the pilot's in-scene track enters mid-pilot (SC11, about 14 minutes), because SC15's climax needs it planted.

### Shot plan

- Changes in this version. (1) SUN. I recomputed it for the story's date, Friday 23 October 2026 (the style bible's VENUS_L03; the evening after PR-36's 10/23/2026 01:47:12 frame), at about 34°N. The true azimuths are 248° at 11° high, 249° at 10° and 9°, 251° at 6.5°, 254° at 3° and 256° at sunset. At those azimuths her shadow misses him (derivation below). So a 260° azimuth cheat is declared for the sunlit phone plates SH01-SH05 and for rig 3a. SH06-SH08 play true, with the disc in the western haze. SH06 is now 'wholly in shade', no longer 'in her shade', and V02-SH02 follows it. SH07's graze is restated at 14° off the glass. S01 plays at a true 249°: the sun sits 21° off Jean's nose and 23° off Venus's, her face is now short-lit, and a frame-right side flag is named. (2) PROP IDs. SH21 drops PR-22: both sacks are rolled shut, and none of their contents is listed. PR-01 in state S7 (a flat 7 cm outline in her right front jeans pocket) is added to SH21, SH20, SH22 and SH24, where her right side or front faces the lens, and to SH19, where it is stated as out of view. SH18 (too small), SH20B (below frame) and SH23 (her left side to the lens) leave it out. (3) NUMBERS. SH12's windscreen mirrors the northern dome about 31° up, not 45°; the conclusions hold. SH17 and V02-SH05 to SH07 are restated at 0.84 m to his eyes. The 0.92 m was the distance to the pad point (x 108, y 100).
- Sun and her shadow, SC01 (derivation). Declination about -11.6°. The true sun by REC time: 00:00:04, 11° at 248°, about 4000K; 00:06:12, 10° at 249°, 3900K; about 00:12 (S01), 9° at 249°, 3700K; 00:21:40, 6.5° at 251°, 3400K; 00:38:15, 3° at 254°, 3000K; 00:52:30, under 0.5° at 256°; 00:53:10, the upper limb on the horizon at 256°. The sun descends about 12° an hour, which holds the timeline's elevations. VENUS'S HEAD: her skull spans car-x 88-106 (centre 97), eye 110 at x 90, crown 121. The low puff at her nape is flattened against the front face of her own pad at x 108, and the pad is about 8 cm deep (assumed; blocker 5). JEAN'S NEAR (LEFT) SIDE, at y about 93, 61 cm east of her head: temple x 94 (104-110 cm), ear x 93-96 (96-102 cm), jaw angle and mole x 95 (90-93 cm), collar points x 91-97 (80-86 cm), back of skull and nape x 97-108. GEOMETRY: a shadow that reaches his plane moves forward by 61 x tan(the sun's angle south of west) and drops by 61/cos(that angle) x tan(elevation). At the true azimuths the forward shift runs from 25 cm (248°) down to 17.5 cm (254°). Her silhouette (x 88-108) therefore lands at x 63-83 at first and x 70-90 at SH05, its rear edge 10 cm to 3 cm ahead of his ear, on the air in front of his face and on his door top. It misses him. DECLARED CHEAT: the azimuth is set to 260° for SH01-SH05 and for rig 3a, while elevation and colour stay true. At 260° the shift is 10.8 cm, so her shadow spans x 77-97 at his plane and covers his temple, ear, jaw angle, mole and collar points. Its rear edge falls just behind his ear, about 73% across the phone frame, and leaves the back of his skull in sun; her pad's shadow takes his nape below about 96 cm. The top edge is 121 - 61.9 x tan(elevation): 109 cm (SH01), 110 cm rising to 113 cm in SH02's lean, 114 cm (SH04) and 118 cm (SH05). The cheat is safe: no frame of SH01-SH08 shows the sun, a horizon or a cast shadow long enough to read its bearing, and the background plates take the same cheated sun. From SH06 the disc sits in the dust haze on the western skyline and its light on the car is a fraction of the sky's, so the element takes its shade pass. The hills 2-5 km east stand above the haze and keep their gold until SH08. SH07's graze stays true: the screen faces 332°, which is 76° off the sun at 256°, so the light arrives 14° off the glass. It reads only as a specular edge on the glossy nail, which is why it can show while Jean's matte skin takes the shade pass.
- S01 sun geometry. At a true 249° and 9° high, the lens axis (155°, tilted down 12°) sits 94° from the sun in azimuth, and about 96° once the tilt and the sun's height are counted. By my count that puts the sun 4-6° behind the lens plane; the note puts it just in front. Either way it is within a few degrees of the plane, where the curve of the front element and the matte-box edge can catch it. That is why a frame-right side flag is named, with 'no flare from the frame-right edge' as the acceptance check. Jean faces 270°, 21° off the sun, which sits on his left. Venus's nose is at 272°, 23° off the sun, also on her left, and the lens is 55° to her right, so her near cheek is the shadow side (short lighting). The screen faces 356°, which is 107° off the sun.
- Carried: SH12's declared high angle (240 cm, tilted down 17°). At about 85 cm both faces would sit behind the glass under a reflected veil, and her hands would drop below the cowl. I use 17° rather than 16° because at 16° the horizon sits on the top edge (the vertical half-angle is 16.13°). At the assumed 27° rake the glass normal sits 27° up and the view ray back to the lens about 22° up, so the glass mirrors the northern dome about 31° up, about 89° from the western afterglow.
- Tilts. Each tilt is computed to hold its stated frame positions: SH15 15° (eyes about 40% down), V02-SH04 16° (eyes about 40%), SH16 15° under its constraint, SH13 12° (her eyes at 38%), SH12 17° (her eyes at 47%, horizon out of frame). With the lens above his eyes, less tilt would drop the eyes lower in frame.
- Header clearance. SH12 at 240 cm: her eye sightline passes 15 cm over the 118 cm header and her chin line 5 cm over it. SH15 at 142 cm: the chin line clears the header by about 3 cm. V02-SH04: the header crosses at about 68%. SH16 at 158 cm: the header is clear down to her neckline. All of this assumes the header sits about 15 cm behind the windscreen base (a rake of about 27°) and about 80 cm in front of her eyes (blocker 5). If the reference model differs, the clearances are the target, not the heights.
- SH16 geometry. Seen from ahead of the car, her neckline and the phone at the dash arc sit only about 4° apart. A frame that shows the hook and hides the phone therefore needs a lens high enough to clear the header (158 cm) and a bottom edge at 26.5°, 1.3° above the top of the phone. 50mm is the widest lens that keeps both faces in and the phone out.
- SH20B geometry. The lens is at (13.1, 11.8), 37 cm above the gravel and 22 cm over the step top, facing 270°, tilted down 14°. The step's near edge (0.5 m out) is 23.7° below the lens and lands about 80% down; the far edge (1.7 m out) is 7.4° below and lands at about 30%. The horizon lands about 7% down. Her toes at about (12.0, 11.58) are 1.13 m out. At T11, focused at 0.9 m, sharp focus runs from 0.65 to 1.5 m; it is a generated plate, so the stop is a depth choice.
- Bag marks. I followed the world bible (PR-05 S7, PR-06 S4) and the treatment: the bags lie west of the latch jamb (x 195-245, y 10-50). The earlier review note put them under the window. Both places are outside the SH25/S02 frame. At the stated 350°, PR-23 lands about 71-77% across and 84-99% down.
- Lucas's 35mm direction (binding) is one film pass over everything. It has fine 35mm negative grain, strongest in the shadows and mids; red-orange halation above about +2.5 stops over mid-grey; a soft highlight shoulder from about +2 stops; a #0A0A0A black floor, deep but not crushed, so Jean's weave holds; film colour that desaturates as highlights climb; and skin hues held within about 3° and never lifted. Depth and perspective come from real S35 focal lengths at stated distances, never from a blur pass. Everything runs at 24 fps with a 180° shutter (1/48 s) and no slow motion. The pass goes on last, natively at each output's resolution, with grain clumps at 0.10% and halation spread at 0.5% of the short side, and the same threshold, shoulder and floor everywhere. V02 is never a crop of graded V01: V02-SH03 and SH04 are regenerated on the rolled S35 gate. The stills take the pass at master size, and S01's pallor lock is measured after the pass.
- Phone versus film, decided on purpose. Her footage stays phone video, because it is her way of seeing him. Inside the phone picture: 26mm-eq deep focus with digital crops, hard-clipped highlights, a drifting AWB, gain noise and compression at dusk, rolling-shutter skew, fingerprint haze and the phone's own shutter; the film pass adds only grain there. Halation, roll-off, S35 depth and 1/48 s blur arrive only at SH12, so the split reads with the sound off. The whole frame is still film: V01's pillars and V02's 3% surround carry FL-BLACK's live grain, and the whole frame (surround and picture as one unit) carries the same gate weave. In V02 the surround opens to full bleed on the two film shots (SH03-SH04) and closes at SH05. Screens seen inside film frames (S01) are plain panels, with the phone picture composited below the halation threshold.
- Look codes. FL-PHONE: AWB about 5000K in gold light, drifting to about 6500K at dusk and locking to about 5600K on the LED; sun at the declared 260° for SH01-SH05, true from SH06. FL-GOLD: balance 5600K, sun 3700K at a true 249°. FL-DUSK: balance 4300K, afterglow 3800K, dome 12000K, no hard source. FL-LANTERN: balance 4300K, lantern 2700K. FL-CANDLE: balance 3200K, flame 1900K, sconces 2400K. FL-BLACK: #0A0A0A with live grain.
- Lens convention. Each lens is given as its full-frame equivalent from the house set, then the real S35 length (about FF / 1.45). Depth of field uses the T-stop as the f-number and a 0.025 mm circle of confusion. Phone fields use the full-frame diagonal (43.3 mm) on the 9:16 picture: SH01's 52mm-eq at 0.85 m gives 0.35 x 0.62 m; SH17's 78mm-eq at 0.84 m gives about 0.23 x 0.41 m; V02's 133mm-eq at 0.84 m gives about 0.13 x 0.24 m. The note's 0.22 x 0.38 m and 0.13 x 0.22 m are within 8% of these. Phone distances are measured to his near surface, as in SH01. Lenses per scene: SC01 uses 35, 50, 65 and 85 (the phone's 26mm-eq is an in-world lens and isn't counted); SC02 uses 29, 35 and 40; SC03 uses 25 and 50; SC08 uses 40.
- Frames, all at 24 fps; stills are logged at 24 with duration 0. V01: 27 shots, 2160 frames (B01 96, B02 144, B03 192, B04 192, B05 192, B06 192, B07 192, B08 144, B09 96, B10 96 = SH20 72 + SH20B 24, B11 192, B12 240, B13 144, B14 48). V02: 8 shots, 360 frames (36+60+24+24+72+48+48+48). No generated plate runs over 5 s.
- Eyes plate map. The phone holds one position for the eyes: wrist on the dash arc at car x 25, y 60, lens at 100 cm, 0.84 m from his eyes (0.92 m to the pad point), aimed at world 154°, 3x crop, LED 5600K, generated at 4K-equivalent. Plate A (5.0 s): the eyes hold at 0.0-3.0 s (V02-SH05) and the laugh runs at 3.0-5.0 s (V02-SH06; V01-SH17 uses 4.0-5.0). Plate C (5.0 s): the settle (V01-SH17 uses 0.0-3.0; V02-SH07 uses 2.6-4.6). No frame appears twice within V02.
- Registered Jean element (SH01-SH06; SH02-SH03 above the collar band; the SH15/V02-SH04 turn). It is one locked pose, rendered as a full-sun pass at each sun height, at the declared 260° azimuth, plus a sky-shade pass. The passes blend through a matte of her shadow that moves 3-5 cm with her laugh jolts and climbs with her lean in SH02 (SH01-SH05); SH06 takes the shade pass alone. His crown curls are one animated layer shared by all passes: they move in every moving shot, go still about 12 frames after the stop in SH02, and lift again in SH03. For SH15 the same element rotates about a neck pivot over a static torso plate.
- Geography. SC01: the road is the axis. Every film setup sits on or west of the centreline, and the change at 45.0 s is cut on action to a centreline position. SH12 and SH13 share one line 10° west of her straight-ahead; SH15, SH16 and V02-SH04 look straight back down the car (heading 180°). In SH15-SH17 his gaze exits frame-right, toward her, never within 10° of the lens. SC02: west is frame-left, except SH20 and SH20B, which sit on the axis looking west; she turns toward frame-right at the end of SH21 and enters frame-left in SH22. SC03: the camera is always south or south-east of the table. In SC01 the camera is handheld only when it is her hand (the phone); every other shot is locked off, with no drone and no push.
- Upstream clashes, named. (a) The head turn: the treatment and world bible turn him 120°, casting says about 60°; I followed the treatment. (b) Venus works from the table's south-east corner, not the treatment's south edge, which would block every south lens. (c) The route's 'clean' look outside the phone becomes film texture. (d) The route's sun flaring on the phone's lens becomes fingerprint haze, because the sun can't reach that lens; S01's sun is flagged off the lens. (e) B09 splits into SH18 and SH19, and B10 into SH20 and SH20B. (f) PR-01 S4 lifts her wrist in B02; I keep it on the arc so SH02 registers. (g) SH22 goes into the dark still laden, where the treatment has her shrug the bags off at the jamb. (h) SH25 opens after a declared elision, and its lens sits in the doorway plane. (i) V02-SH03 and SH04 are regenerated rather than cut. (j) S01 is set on a leg V01 never shows, from behind Jean's right shoulder, with the phone turned about 110°. (k) The SH12-SH14 plate boundaries differ from the treatment's; each plate is 5 s or less. (l) In SH16 the phone lift plays below frame, pending blocker 8. (m) SH12's 2.4 m height is my choice (blocker 9). (n) The treatment and route place the sun as the style bible's date gives it; its azimuth is cheated only for SH01-SH05 and rig 3a, and declared.
- Dead-eye light: no source sits near any lens that sees his eyes, so the corneas carry a dull sheen (SH15, V02-SH04) or a dull LED smear (SH17, V02-SH05 to SH07), never a crisp catchlight.
- Taste notes. Hands before faces is followed: her face first appears at SH12, and his only at SH15 and inside her phone. S01 breaks it on purpose, because a poster needs its lead. One real detail per scene: the stop sign (SC01), the worn track and the unbroken step (SC02), the printer (SC03). Restraint over explanation: her shadow climbing him in SH01-SH05 and her crown inside C13-R1 in SH23 carry the idea with no words. The craft rules are broken on purpose twice. Face close-ups sit at 0.84-0.85 m, because it is her phone at arm's length. The phone UI follows the label rule: a blank screen in the plate, with the art composited later (clearance pending, A10).
- IDs. PR-10, PR-11 and PR-58 are LOC-02 props that appear in LOC-01 shots, because the location is the moving car. PR-04 and PR-01 appear in LOC-03 and LOC-04 shots on Venus, as the world bible lists them (PR-04 S5, PR-01 S7). No new character, look, location or prop is introduced; the chin form, padlock panel and step form are rig elements, not story props. Lucas's reference images were not inspected; they are used only as the brief describes them, and no character is modelled on any face.
- Blocker (Producer and generation supervisor): The practical plates need a stage day. Everything else is generated from virtual positions, so no rigged car and no road-safety sign-off are needed. → LIGHT AND CAMERA PER RIG. Crew and schedule are the producer's. The light states below are listed in an order that keeps each rig to one change. RIG 1, GREEN PHONE: a blank phone on green at the phone's pose, the camera square to the glass and framing the full screen height. State A: a 6500K panel under the glass (screen glow) plus a soft 12000K top on the nail, 3:1. Shoot B2-V01 (ends on the bottom-bar icon, SH17) and B2-V02 (enters the bottom edge about 78% across and rests on V02's icon mark, V02-SH07), both long with handles. State B: state A plus a small hard 2700K source about 14° off the glass (the true 256° sun against the screen's 332°), from frame-right and a little behind camera, flagged so it shows only as a thin specular edge on the nail ridge and knuckle crease. Shoot B1 (V01-SH07). RIG 2, TABLETOP (a LOC-05 counter section, top 107 cm): S03. The live candle (about 1900K) is the key beyond the house, with the red neon practical and a soft 2400K fill behind camera, 8:1. Camera rolled for 4:5 at 225 cm, 55 cm east of the house, down 65°, 40mm FF-eq, T2.8. RIG 3, LOC-01 BUCK at world-bible heights. 3a, SH02-SH03: a hard 3900K sun from the driver side, 10° high at the declared 260° cheat (10° rearward of square; the true sun is 249°), plus a soft 10000K top. The hand double sits with her skull at car-x 88-106, eye 110, so her own shadow falls on the form's ear, jaw and collar points (car-x 77-97 at his plane). Camera at the phone's lens point (dash arc, 100 cm, world 152°, down 5°, 52mm-eq). SH-3 is dusted to C1 on a P1 head-and-shoulders form. 3b: kill the sun; set a 12000K dome and a large soft 3800K source at the west front, 4:1. SH14 at the SH13/SH14 position (135 cm, 1.4 m, 35mm FF-eq), with a still Jean double seen from the back of the head only. SH15 finger plate at the SH15 position (centreline, 1.6 m, 142 cm, down 15°, 85mm FF-eq) on a matched chin form, turned by a motion-control rotator playing the turn curve, with PR-04 hanging from the hand double's thumb and ring finger; also shot on the rolled gate for V02-SH04. 3c: fly a black roof flag at 260 cm and keep one large soft source from the south rear, 3:1. SH19 from a jib remote head at 245 cm, down 67°, 29mm FF-eq, with the inert PR-02 replica. RIG 4, PARTIAL LOC-04 SET: wall A with PR-15, its exterior dressed as the frontage with the patch and the track, plus the table, lantern, chairs and the lower strip of wall C. One soft 12000K source outside wall A throughout. 4a, SH22 doorway pass f20-f95: the SH22 tripod point, 100 cm, 350°, 35mm FF-eq, lantern off. 4b, SH23: (240, 160), 160 cm, 347°, down 17°, 50mm FF-eq, with PR-16 switched on in shot. 4c, SH24: a low mount at 55 cm, 325°, 50mm FF-eq. 4d, the SH20 padlock element: a boarded plywood panel with a hasp and a real brass padlock; camera 2.3 m out and 35 cm up, looking up at the lock as in SH20, 29mm FF-eq; a soft 12000K source from the south on the panel face and a bright soft 3800K backdrop to the west, 3:1. 4e, SH20B fallback only: a 120 x 40 x 15 cm concrete step form against a stucco flat, dressed with sifted caliche raked into wind ripples, with the Venus body double's boots at its south edge. Camera 37 cm up, 0.5 m off its east end, facing along it, down 14°, 35mm FF-eq, T11. A low, bright, soft 3800K backdrop beyond its west end rakes the ripples, with a soft 12000K top from the south, 3:1.
- Blocker (Producer): Rights and consents for the practical plates are not in hand, and none is claimed as cleared. → Obtain, log and sign each item; each stays pending until signed. (1) Hand double: SH02-SH03, SH07 (B1), SH14, the SH15 finger plate, SH17 (B2-V01), the SH20 padlock element, V02-SH01, V02-SH04, V02-SH07 (B2-V02) and S03. (2) Venus body double: SH19, the SH22 doorway pass, SH23, SH24, and the SH20B fallback if it is used. (3) Jean still double in dead-state makeup: SH14 and SH19. The scope for all three is V01, V02, S01-S03 and pitch circulation, including compositing with the generated characters. (4) The armourer's sign-off for SH19. (5) Fire-safety sign-off for S03. The head form, chin form and step form need no release.
- Blocker (Generation supervisor): A still subject under moving light and moving hair (SH01, SH02-SH03 above the collar band, SH04-SH06), and a head turned by a hand (SH15, V02-SH04). A generator is likely to move Jean's head with the shadow or the wind, redraw him between jump cuts, or let the fingers slide or sink on the turn. → Build the registered Jean element with its sun passes (at the declared 260° azimuth) and its shade pass, the moving shadow matte (SH01-SH05; SH06 takes the shade pass only) and one shared curl layer. For the turn, build a keyed rotation about a neck pivot over a static torso plate, composited with the rig-3b finger plate. Acceptance: across SH01-SH11, no pixel of his head, face, collar or sunglasses moves (above the collar band only, in SH02-SH03), and they overlay within a few pixels across every cut, including V02-SH01 to SH02. Her shadow lands on his temple, ear, jaw and collar points, with its rear edge just behind his ear. In SH15 the fingertips hold contact within a few pixels, the glasses stay one pair hanging from her hand, and the head stops at P2 with no overshoot. Fallback for SH15: ease P1 to about 80° right.
- Blocker (Generation supervisor, producer and finishing): Generation resolution: V02's punch-in on the eyes plate needs a 4K-equivalent source, and the stills' master sizes are not set. → Generate plates A and C at 4K-equivalent or better, so the dull corneas and the dust on the lashes hold at V02's punch (a field of about 0.13 x 0.24 m at 0.84 m). Name the master sizes for S01, S02 and S03; grain and halation are defined as fractions of each still's short side.
- Blocker (Production designer): Several LOC-01 dimensions are unspecified: the headrest pads' width and depth, and the windscreen's rake and header position. S01's clearance of at least 6 cm for his profile past the pad, the header positions in SH12, SH15, SH16 and V02-SH04, and the fore-aft landing of her shadow in SH01-SH05 all rest on assumed values: pad depth about 8 cm, header 15 cm behind the windscreen base and about 80 cm in front of her eyes, rake about 27°. → State the pads' width and depth and the header's position and rake on the reference model. The DP then checks S01, moving further outboard (never higher) if the clearance falls under 6 cm. The DP holds the header clearances by adjusting lens height and tilt; in SH12 the target stays the header at her collar with her eyes at least 10 cm clear. The DP re-derives the shadow's rear edge; the target stays just behind his ear.
- Blocker (Director, with casting): The head turn at 45.0 s: the treatment and world bible turn his head 120° to P2 (compass 330°), while casting writes about 60°. SH15-SH17, SC02 and V02-SH04 to SH07 are staged on 120°. → Confirm 120° and correct the casting text. If 60° is chosen, the DP restages SH15-SH17, because at 60° his eyes would not face her phone on the dash arc.
- Blocker (Director): Blind screen. On the dash arc, aimed at world 152-154°, the phone's screen faces 332-334°, while Venus sits at about 198° from the phone, 134° behind its screen, so she cannot see it. This affects both thumb rests (V01-SH07, SH17 and V02-SH07). The route's pang, her thumb resting over his open eyes ('not like this, not yet'), then reads as a blind reflex. → Choose one. (A) Accept a blind thumb: she knows the icon's place by feel. The picture never shows which way the screen faces, and SH16 is already framed so the phone's lift and landing stay below frame. (B) Upstream moves the phone to her eyeline, for example held at chest height in her right hand with the screen toward her and the lens still on him, and re-aims P2 so his eyes still sit frontal in it. The DP then re-registers SH01-SH11 and SH17, re-derives her shadow for the new wrist and restages SH16 to show the lift.
- Blocker (Director): Departures from the treatment that still need the director's approval. Already approved: the SH02 sign (now the world's), the SH10 bump join, the SH18 spring rock, SH20 on the axis, SH22 at the left third, SH24 at 325°, the SC03 hard cut, V02-B04's static punch-in and V02-SH07's f0 entry. The returned SH23 ruling is fixed as asked. → Approve or send back each of these. NEW: the 260° sun-azimuth cheat for SH01-SH05 and rig 3a (true 248-254°), without which her shadow misses him; SH06 now plays wholly in shade, with the sun in the western haze, rather than in her shade. CARRIED: SH12's lens at 240 cm, tilted down 17°, a declared high angle. B10 split into SH20 (60.0-63.0 s) and the SH20B insert (63.0-64.0 s). Her wrist stays on the dash arc through B02. B09 split into SH18 and SH19. SH21's tripod at (25.6, 12.2), ending on her turn with the sacks hugged low under PR-04. SH22 goes into the dark still laden. SH23's camera at 160 cm, down 17°, with her crown in C13-R1. SH25 opens after a declared elision, lens in the doorway plane. SH15's lens raised to 142 cm. SH16 reframed (50mm, 158 cm, heading 180°), with the phone lift below frame. SH20's padlock and hands as a practical element. V02-SH03 and SH04 regenerated. A 2.6 s elision from V02-SH06 to SH07. The SH12-SH14 plate boundaries. None of these changes a beat's frame count.
- Blocker (Lucas): S01 has three options on one rig. All share the declared viewpoint change (behind his right shoulder, outboard) and the two phone cheats: the phone turned about 110° to face 356°, 20° off our axis, and its picture taken from the dash arc. The plan specifies option C. → Choose one option and approve the viewpoint and phone cheats with it. (C, planned) The southbound leg at REC about 00:12: the sun 9° high at a true 249°, 21° off his nose and 23° off hers, gold across the front planes of his profile, her face short-lit, and no azimuth cheat. The sun sits within a few degrees of the lens plane, so a frame-right side flag is named, with no flare from that edge as its acceptance. Its costs: a one-off phone frame, no shadow of her on him, and the risk that in full gold he may read alive at thumbnail size. The chroma lock is its acceptance test: his sunlit cheek at no more than half her chroma after the film pass, grey-ash lips and lids at C* 8 or under, and no warming of him in the grade. (A) Northbound at 00:21:40, under the same declared 260° azimuth cheat as the phone plates: Jean in her shadow, reading grey by light alone, with the screen carrying V01-SH01's frame. The cost is less gold on him and a cheat carried into the key art. (B) A sun cheated to 240°, 22° high, on the northbound leg: it lights him but reads as mid-afternoon, and it carries the same pallor risk as C.
